/// file: shader/absmax.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer B0 { float x[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 1) buffer B1 { uint amax[]; };
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint n;
    uint slot;
};

shared float red[256];

void main() {
    uint lid = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
    uint stride = gl_NumWorkGroups.x * 256u;
    float m = 0.0;
    for (uint i = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x; i < n; i += stride) {
        m = max(m, abs(x[i]));
    }
    red[lid] = m;
    barrier();
    for (uint s = 128u; s > 0u; s >>= 1) {
        if (lid < s) {
            red[lid] = max(red[lid], red[lid + s]);
        }
        barrier();
    }
    if (lid == 0u) {
        atomicMax(amax[slot], floatBitsToUint(red[0]));
    }
}

/// file: shader/adaln.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_EXT_shader_atomic_float : require
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer buf_x {
    float x[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer buf_ada {
    float ada[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) buffer buf_y {
    float y[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 3) writeonly buffer buf_dx {
    float dx[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 4) buffer buf_dada {
    float dada[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint bt;
    uint d;
    uint seqlen;
    uint op;
    uint stride;
    uint s_off;
    uint sh_off;
};

void main() {
    uint idx = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    if (idx < bt * d) {
        uint col = idx % d;
        uint base = idx / d / seqlen * stride;
        float sc = ada[base + s_off + col];
        if (op == 0u) {
            y[idx] = x[idx] * (1.0 + sc) + ada[base + sh_off + col];
        } else {
            float g = y[idx];
            dx[idx] = g * (1.0 + sc);
            atomicAdd(dada[base + s_off + col], g * x[idx]);
            atomicAdd(dada[base + sh_off + col], g);
        }
    }
}

/// file: shader/adamw.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_EXT_shader_explicit_arithmetic_types_float16 : require
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) buffer buf_w {
    float w[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer buf_g {
    float g[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) buffer buf_m {
    float m[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 3) buffer buf_v {
    float v[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 4) writeonly buffer buf_w16 {
    float16_t w16[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 5) readonly buffer buf_opt {
    float lr;
    float bc1;
    float bc2;
    float norm;
    float scale;
    float gmul;
    float good;
    float skip;
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint n;
    float beta1;
    float beta2;
    float eps;
    float wd;
};

void main() {
    uint i = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    if (i < n && skip == 0.0) {
        float gi = g[i] * scale;
        float mi = beta1 * m[i] + (1.0 - beta1) * gi;
        float vi = beta2 * v[i] + (1.0 - beta2) * gi * gi;
        m[i] = mi;
        v[i] = vi;
        float w0 = w[i];
        float wi = w0 - lr * ((mi / bc1) / (sqrt(vi / bc2) + eps) + wd * w0);
        w[i] = wi;
        w16[i] = float16_t(wi);
    }
}

/// file: shader/argmax.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;
layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer L { float logits[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 1) writeonly buffer T { uint tok[]; };
layout(push_constant) uniform pc { uint rows; uint vocab; float temp; uint seed; };
shared float sval[256];
shared uint sidx[256];

uint hash(uint x) {
    uint s = x * 747796405u + 2891336453u;
    uint w = ((s >> ((s >> 28u) + 4u)) ^ s) * 277803737u;
    return (w >> 22u) ^ w;
}

float gumbel_key(float logit, uint rs, uint v) {
    if (temp > 0.0) {
        float u = (float(hash(rs ^ v) >> 8u) + 0.5) / 16777216.0;
        return logit / temp - log(-log(u));
    } else {
        return logit;
    }
}

void main() {
    uint row = gl_WorkGroupID.x;
    if (row < rows) {
        uint tid = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
        uint base = row * vocab;
        uint rs = hash(seed ^ hash(row));
        float best = -1e30;
        uint bi = 0;
        for (uint v = tid; v < vocab; v += 256u) {
            float key = gumbel_key(logits[base + v], rs, v);
            if (key > best) {
                best = key;
                bi = v;
            }
        }
        sval[tid] = best;
        sidx[tid] = bi;
        barrier();
        for (uint stride = 128u; stride > 0u; stride = stride / 2u) {
            if (tid < stride && sval[tid + stride] > sval[tid]) {
                sval[tid] = sval[tid + stride];
                sidx[tid] = sidx[tid + stride];
            }
            barrier();
        }
        if (tid == 0u) {
            tok[row] = sidx[0];
        }
    }
}

/// file: shader/cast_f16.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_EXT_shader_explicit_arithmetic_types_float16 : require

layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer buf_src {
    float src[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) writeonly buffer buf_dst {
    float16_t dst[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) readonly buffer buf_amax {
    uint amax[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint n;
    uint slot;
};

float slot_scale(uint s) {
    if (s == ~0u) {
        return 1.0;
    } else if (!(uintBitsToFloat(amax[s]) > 0.0) || isinf(uintBitsToFloat(amax[s]))) {
        return 1.0;
    } else {
        return exp2(141.0 - float((amax[s] >> 23) & 0xffu));
    }
}

void main() {
    uint i = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    if (i < n) {
        dst[i] = float16_t(src[i] * slot_scale(slot));
    }
}

/// file: shader/elem.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) buffer buf_x {
    float x[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) buffer buf_pre {
    float pre[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) readonly buffer buf_up {
    float up[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 3) readonly buffer buf_gate {
    float gate[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 4) writeonly buffer buf_dup {
    float dup[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint n;
    uint op;
};

float sigmoidf(float v) {
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-v));
}

void main() {
    uint i = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    if (i < n && op == 0) {
        float v = x[i];
        pre[i] = v;
        x[i] = v * sigmoidf(v);
    } else if (i < n) {
        float dh = x[i];
        float v = pre[i];
        float sg = sigmoidf(v);
        dup[i] = dh * gate[i];
        x[i] = dh * up[i] * (sg + v * sg * (1.0 - sg));
    }
}

/// file: shader/embed.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_EXT_shader_atomic_float : require
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer buf_ids {
    uint ids[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) buffer buf_tok {
    float tok_emb[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) buffer buf_mask {
    float mask_emb[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 3) buffer buf_out {
    float outp[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint bt;
    uint d;
    uint op;
    uint vocab;
};

void main() {
    uint idx = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    uint row = idx / d;
    uint col = idx % d;
    if (idx < bt * d) {
        uint tok = ids[row];
        if (op == 0u && tok == vocab) {
            outp[idx] = mask_emb[col];
        } else if (op == 0u) {
            outp[idx] = tok_emb[tok * d + col];
        } else if (tok == vocab) {
            atomicAdd(mask_emb[col], outp[idx]);
        } else {
            atomicAdd(tok_emb[tok * d + col], outp[idx]);
        }
    }
}

/// file: shader/headnorm.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer B0 { float x[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 1) buffer B1 { float y[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 2) buffer B2 { float rms[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 3) writeonly buffer B3 { float dx[]; };
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint n_vec;
    uint op;
};

shared float red[256];

float group_sum(float v, uint lid) {
    red[lid] = v;
    barrier();
    for (uint s = 32u; s > 0u; s >>= 1) {
        if ((lid & 63u) < s) {
            red[lid] += red[lid + s];
        }
        barrier();
    }
    return red[lid & ~63u];
}

void main() {
    uint lid = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
    uint vec = gl_WorkGroupID.x * 4u + (lid >> 6);
    bool live = vec < n_vec;
    uint i = vec * 64u + (lid & 63u);
    float xi = 0.0;
    if (live) {
        xi = x[i];
    }
    if (op == 0u) {
        float r = sqrt(group_sum(xi * xi, lid) / 64.0 + 1e-5);
        if (live) {
            y[i] = xi / r;
            if ((lid & 63u) == 0u) {
                rms[vec] = r;
            }
        }
    } else {
        float dyi = 0.0;
        if (live) {
            dyi = y[i];
        }
        float s = group_sum(xi * dyi, lid);
        if (live) {
            float r = rms[vec];
            dx[i] = dyi / r - xi * s / (64.0 * r * r * r);
        }
    }
}

/// file: shader/heads_bwd.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer B0 { float DK[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer B1 { float DV[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 2) readonly buffer B2 { float DR[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 3) readonly buffer B3 { float DW[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 4) readonly buffer B4 { float DA[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 5) readonly buffer B5 { float DK2[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 6) readonly buffer B6 { float DV2[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 7) readonly buffer B7 { float DR2[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 8) readonly buffer B8 { float DW2[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 9) readonly buffer B9 { float DA2[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 10) readonly buffer B10 { float khat[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 11) readonly buffer B11 { float kinv[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 12) readonly buffer B12 { float PW[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 13) readonly buffer B13 { float PA[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 14) writeonly buffer B14 { float OK[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 15) writeonly buffer B15 { float OV[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 16) writeonly buffer B16 { float OR[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 17) writeonly buffer B17 { float OW[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 18) writeonly buffer B18 { float OA[]; };
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint n_vec;
    uint hd;
    uint seqlen;
};

const float C = 0.60653066;

shared float red[256];

float group_sum(float x, uint lid) {
    red[lid] = x;
    barrier();
    for (uint s = 32u; s > 0u; s >>= 1) {
        if ((lid & 63u) < s) {
            red[lid] += red[lid + s];
        }
        barrier();
    }
    return red[lid & ~63u];
}

void main() {
    uint lid = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
    uint vec = gl_WorkGroupID.x * 4u + (lid >> 6);
    uint e = lid & 63u;
    bool live = vec < n_vec;
    uint t = vec % seqlen;
    uint bh = vec / seqlen;
    uint ui = ((bh / hd) * seqlen + t) * hd * 64u + (bh % hd) * 64u + e;
    uint pi = vec * 64u + e;
    float dk = 0.0;
    float kh = 0.0;
    if (live) {
        dk = 0.5 * (DK[pi] + DK2[pi]);
        kh = khat[pi];
    }
    float dot = group_sum(kh * dk, lid);
    if (live) {
        OK[ui] = (dk - kh * dot) * kinv[vec];
        OV[ui] = 0.5 * (DV[pi] + DV2[pi]);
        OR[ui] = 0.5 * (DR[pi] + DR2[pi]);
        float w = PW[pi];
        float s = -log(w) / C;
        OW[ui] = 0.5 * (DW[pi] + DW2[pi]) * (-C * w * s * (1.0 - s));
        float a = PA[pi];
        OA[ui] = 0.5 * (DA[pi] + DA2[pi]) * a * (1.0 - a);
    }
}

/// file: shader/heads_fwd.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer B0 { float K[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer B1 { float V[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 2) readonly buffer B2 { float R[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 3) readonly buffer B3 { float W[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 4) readonly buffer B4 { float A[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 5) writeonly buffer B5 { float PK[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 6) writeonly buffer B6 { float PV[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 7) writeonly buffer B7 { float PR[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 8) writeonly buffer B8 { float PW[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 9) writeonly buffer B9 { float PA[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 10) writeonly buffer B10 { float kinv[]; };
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint n_vec;
    uint hd;
    uint seqlen;
};

const float C = 0.60653066;

shared float red[256];

float group_sum(float x, uint lid) {
    red[lid] = x;
    barrier();
    for (uint s = 32u; s > 0u; s >>= 1) {
        if ((lid & 63u) < s) {
            red[lid] += red[lid + s];
        }
        barrier();
    }
    return red[lid & ~63u];
}

void main() {
    uint lid = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
    uint vec = gl_WorkGroupID.x * 4u + (lid >> 6);
    uint e = lid & 63u;
    bool live = vec < n_vec;
    uint t = vec % seqlen;
    uint bh = vec / seqlen;
    uint ui = ((bh / hd) * seqlen + t) * hd * 64u + (bh % hd) * 64u + e;
    uint pi = vec * 64u + e;
    float k = 0.0;
    if (live) {
        k = K[ui];
    }
    float scale = 1.0 / (sqrt(group_sum(k * k, lid)) + 1e-6);
    if (live) {
        PK[pi] = k * scale;
        if (e == 0u) {
            kinv[vec] = scale;
        }
        PV[pi] = V[ui];
        PR[pi] = R[ui];
        PW[pi] = exp(-C / (1.0 + exp(-W[ui])));
        PA[pi] = 1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-A[ui]));
    }
}

/// file: shader/matmul.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer buf_a {
    float a[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer buf_b {
    float b[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) writeonly buffer buf_c {
    float c[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint m;
    uint n;
    uint k;
};

void main() {
    uint row = gl_GlobalInvocationID.y;
    uint col = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    if (row < m && col < n) {
        float acc = 0.0;
        for (uint i = 0; i < k; i += 1u) {
            acc += a[row * k + i] * b[i * n + col];
        }
        c[row * n + col] = acc;
    }
}

/// file: shader/matmul_bw.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer buf_a {
    float a[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer buf_b {
    float b[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) readonly buffer buf_dc {
    float dc[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 3) writeonly buffer buf_dout {
    float dout[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint m;
    uint n;
    uint k;
    uint mode;
};

void main() {
    uint row = gl_GlobalInvocationID.y;
    uint col = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    if (mode == 0 && row < m && col < k) {
        float acc = 0.0;
        for (uint j = 0; j < n; j += 1u) {
            acc += dc[row * n + j] * b[col * n + j];
        }
        dout[row * k + col] = acc;
    } else if (mode != 0 && row < k && col < n) {
        float acc = 0.0;
        for (uint i = 0; i < m; i += 1u) {
            acc += a[i * k + row] * dc[i * n + col];
        }
        dout[row * n + col] = acc;
    }
}

/// file: shader/matmul_f16.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_KHR_cooperative_matrix : require
#extension GL_KHR_memory_scope_semantics : require
#extension GL_EXT_shader_explicit_arithmetic_types_float16 : require
#extension GL_KHR_shader_subgroup_basic : require
layout(local_size_x = 128) in;
layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer BA { float16_t A[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer BB { float16_t B[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 2) buffer BC { float C[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 3) readonly buffer BS { uint amax[]; };
layout(push_constant) uniform pc { uint m, n, k, sa, sb, acc; };
const uint T = 16;
float slot_scale(uint s) {
    if (s == ~0u) {
        return 1.0;
    } else if (!(uintBitsToFloat(amax[s]) > 0.0) || isinf(uintBitsToFloat(amax[s]))) {
        return 1.0;
    } else {
        return exp2(141.0 - float((amax[s] >> 23) & 0xffu));
    }
}
void main() {
    uint sg = gl_SubgroupID;
    uint row0 = gl_WorkGroupID.y * 64u + sg * 16u;
    uint col0 = gl_WorkGroupID.x * 64u;
    coopmat<float, gl_ScopeSubgroup, T, T, gl_MatrixUseAccumulator> ac[4];
    for (uint i = 0; i < 4u; i = i + 1) {
        ac[i] = coopmat<float, gl_ScopeSubgroup, T, T, gl_MatrixUseAccumulator>(0.0);
    }
    for (uint kk = 0; kk < k; kk = kk + T) {
        coopmat<float16_t, gl_ScopeSubgroup, T, T, gl_MatrixUseA> ma;
        coopMatLoad(ma, A, row0 * k + kk, k, gl_CooperativeMatrixLayoutRowMajor);
        for (uint t = 0; t < 4u; t = t + 1) {
            coopmat<float16_t, gl_ScopeSubgroup, T, T, gl_MatrixUseB> mb;
            coopMatLoad(mb, B, kk * n + col0 + t * 16u, n, gl_CooperativeMatrixLayoutRowMajor);
            ac[t] = coopMatMulAdd(ma, mb, ac[t]);
        }
    }
    float inv = 1.0 / (slot_scale(sa) * slot_scale(sb));
    for (uint t = 0; t < 4u; t = t + 1) {
        uint at = row0 * n + col0 + t * 16u;
        ac[t] = ac[t] * inv;
        if (acc != 0u) {
            coopmat<float, gl_ScopeSubgroup, T, T, gl_MatrixUseAccumulator> prev;
            coopMatLoad(prev, C, at, n, gl_CooperativeMatrixLayoutRowMajor);
            ac[t] = ac[t] + prev;
        }
        coopMatStore(ac[t], C, at, n, gl_CooperativeMatrixLayoutRowMajor);
    }
}

/// file: shader/matmul_f16_wide.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_KHR_cooperative_matrix : require
#extension GL_KHR_memory_scope_semantics : require
#extension GL_EXT_shader_explicit_arithmetic_types_float16 : require
#extension GL_KHR_shader_subgroup_basic : require
layout(local_size_x = 128) in;
layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer BA { float16_t A[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer BB { float16_t B[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 2) buffer BC { float C[]; };
layout(std430, binding = 3) readonly buffer BS { uint amax[]; };
layout(push_constant) uniform pc { uint m, n, k, sa, sb, acc; };
const uint T = 16;
float slot_scale(uint s) {
    if (s == ~0u) {
        return 1.0;
    } else if (!(uintBitsToFloat(amax[s]) > 0.0) || isinf(uintBitsToFloat(amax[s]))) {
        return 1.0;
    } else {
        return exp2(141.0 - float((amax[s] >> 23) & 0xffu));
    }
}
void main() {
    uint sg = gl_SubgroupID;
    uint row0 = gl_WorkGroupID.y * 256u + sg * 64u;
    uint col0 = gl_WorkGroupID.x * 64u;
    coopmat<float, gl_ScopeSubgroup, T, T, gl_MatrixUseAccumulator> ac[4][4];
    for (uint i = 0; i < 4u; i = i + 1) {
        for (uint j = 0; j < 4u; j = j + 1) {
            ac[i][j] = coopmat<float, gl_ScopeSubgroup, T, T, gl_MatrixUseAccumulator>(0.0);
        }
    }
    for (uint kk = 0; kk < k; kk = kk + T) {
        coopmat<float16_t, gl_ScopeSubgroup, T, T, gl_MatrixUseA> a[4];
        for (uint i = 0; i < 4u; i = i + 1) {
            coopMatLoad(a[i], A, (row0 + i * 16u) * k + kk, k, gl_CooperativeMatrixLayoutRowMajor);
        }
        for (uint t = 0; t < 4u; t = t + 1) {
            coopmat<float16_t, gl_ScopeSubgroup, T, T, gl_MatrixUseB> mb;
            coopMatLoad(mb, B, kk * n + col0 + t * 16u, n, gl_CooperativeMatrixLayoutRowMajor);
            for (uint i = 0; i < 4u; i = i + 1) {
                ac[i][t] = coopMatMulAdd(a[i], mb, ac[i][t]);
            }
        }
    }
    float inv = 1.0 / (slot_scale(sa) * slot_scale(sb));
    for (uint i = 0; i < 4u; i = i + 1) {
        for (uint t = 0; t < 4u; t = t + 1) {
            uint at = (row0 + i * 16u) * n + col0 + t * 16u;
            ac[i][t] = ac[i][t] * inv;
            if (acc != 0u) {
                coopmat<float, gl_ScopeSubgroup, T, T, gl_MatrixUseAccumulator> prev;
                coopMatLoad(prev, C, at, n, gl_CooperativeMatrixLayoutRowMajor);
                ac[i][t] = ac[i][t] + prev;
            }
            coopMatStore(ac[i][t], C, at, n, gl_CooperativeMatrixLayoutRowMajor);
        }
    }
}

/// file: shader/muon.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_EXT_shader_explicit_arithmetic_types_float16 : require
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) buffer buf_a {
    float a[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) buffer buf_b {
    float b[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) buffer buf_x {
    float x[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 3) writeonly buffer buf_h {
    float16_t h[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 4) buffer buf_red {
    float red[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 5) readonly buffer buf_opt {
    float lr;
    float bc1;
    float bc2;
    float norm;
    float scale;
    float gmul;
    float good;
    float skip;
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint n;
    uint op;
    uint aux;
    float p0;
    float p1;
};

shared float sh[256];

float reduce_sum(float v, uint lid) {
    sh[lid] = v;
    barrier();
    for (uint s = 128u; s > 0u; s >>= 1u) {
        if (lid < s) {
            sh[lid] += sh[lid + s];
        }
        barrier();
    }
    return sh[0];
}

void momentum(uint i, uint lid) {
    float xi = 0.0;
    if (i < n && skip == 0.0) {
        float g = a[i] * scale;
        float m = p0 * b[i] + g;
        b[i] = m;
        xi = g + p0 * m;
        x[i] = xi;
    }
    float s = reduce_sum(xi * xi, lid);
    if (lid == 0u) {
        red[gl_WorkGroupID.x] = s;
    }
}

void norm_inv(uint lid) {
    float acc = 0.0;
    for (uint j = lid; j < n; j += 256u) {
        acc += red[j];
    }
    float s = reduce_sum(acc, lid);
    if (lid == 0u) {
        red[aux] = 1.0 / (sqrt(s) + 1e-7);
    }
}

void elementwise(uint i) {
    switch (op) {
    case 2u:
        x[i] = x[i] * red[aux];
        h[i] = float16_t(x[i]);
        break;
    case 3u:
        h[i] = float16_t(p0 * a[i] + p1 * b[i]);
        break;
    case 4u:
        x[i] = p0 * x[i] + a[i];
        h[i] = float16_t(x[i]);
        break;
    default:
        if (skip == 0.0) {
            a[i] = a[i] - lr * p0 * x[i];
            h[i] = float16_t(a[i]);
        }
        break;
    }
}

void main() {
    uint i = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    uint lid = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
    switch (op) {
    case 0u:
        momentum(i, lid);
        break;
    case 1u:
        norm_inv(lid);
        break;
    default:
        if (i < n) {
            elementwise(i);
        }
        break;
    }
}

/// file: shader/repack.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer buf_in {
    float src[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) writeonly buffer buf_out {
    float dst[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint bt;
    uint hd;
    uint dh;
    uint seqlen;
    uint op;
};

void main() {
    uint i = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    uint D = hd * dh;
    uint row = i / D;
    uint c = i % D;
    uint packed = ((row / seqlen * hd + c / dh) * seqlen + row % seqlen) * dh + c % dh;
    if (i < bt * D && op == 0) {
        dst[packed] = src[i];
    } else if (i < bt * D) {
        dst[i] = src[packed];
    }
}

/// file: shader/rmsnorm.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_EXT_shader_atomic_float : require
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer buf_x {
    float x[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer buf_g {
    float g[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) buffer buf_y {
    float y[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 3) buffer buf_rms {
    float rms_buf[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 4) buffer buf_dg {
    float dg[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 5) buffer buf_dacc {
    float dacc[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint rows;
    uint d;
    uint op;
};

shared float red[256];

float reduce_sum(float v, uint lid) {
    red[lid] = v;
    barrier();
    for (uint o = 128; o > 0; o >>= 1) {
        if (lid < o) {
            red[lid] += red[lid + o];
        }
        barrier();
    }
    float r = red[0];
    barrier();
    return r;
}

void forward(uint row, uint lid, uint base) {
    float ss = 0.0;
    for (uint i = lid; i < d; i += 256) {
        ss += x[base + i] * x[base + i];
    }
    float r = sqrt(reduce_sum(ss, lid) / float(d) + 1e-5);
    if (lid == 0) {
        rms_buf[row] = r;
    }
    for (uint i = lid; i < d; i += 256) {
        y[base + i] = x[base + i] / r * g[i];
    }
}

void backward(uint row, uint lid, uint base) {
    float r = rms_buf[row];
    float part = 0.0;
    for (uint i = lid; i < d; i += 256) {
        part += x[base + i] * g[i] * y[base + i];
    }
    float s = reduce_sum(part, lid);
    for (uint i = lid; i < d; i += 256) {
        float dyi = y[base + i];
        float xi = x[base + i];
        dacc[base + i] += (g[i] * dyi) / r - xi / (r * r * r * float(d)) * s;
        atomicAdd(dg[i], xi / r * dyi);
    }
}

void main() {
    uint row = gl_WorkGroupID.x;
    uint lid = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
    if (row < rows && op == 0) {
        forward(row, lid, row * d);
    } else if (row < rows) {
        backward(row, lid, row * d);
    }
}

/// file: shader/softmax_ce.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_EXT_shader_atomic_float : require
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) buffer buf_logits {
    float logits[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer buf_target {
    uint target[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) readonly buffer buf_mask {
    float mask[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 3) readonly buffer buf_weight {
    float weight[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 4) buffer buf_loss {
    float loss[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 5) readonly buffer buf_opt {
    float lr;
    float bc1;
    float bc2;
    float norm;
    float scale;
    float gmul;
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint rows;
    uint vocab;
};

shared float red[256];

float reduce_max(float v, uint lid) {
    red[lid] = v;
    barrier();
    for (uint o = 128; o > 0; o >>= 1) {
        if (lid < o) {
            red[lid] = max(red[lid], red[lid + o]);
        }
        barrier();
    }
    float r = red[0];
    barrier();
    return r;
}

float reduce_sum(float v, uint lid) {
    red[lid] = v;
    barrier();
    for (uint o = 128; o > 0; o >>= 1) {
        if (lid < o) {
            red[lid] += red[lid + o];
        }
        barrier();
    }
    float r = red[0];
    barrier();
    return r;
}

void row_ce(uint row, uint lid) {
    uint base = row * vocab;
    float part_mx = -1e30;
    for (uint i = lid; i < vocab; i += 256) {
        part_mx = max(part_mx, logits[base + i]);
    }
    float mx = reduce_max(part_mx, lid);
    float part_se = 0.0;
    for (uint i = lid; i < vocab; i += 256) {
        part_se += exp(logits[base + i] - mx);
    }
    float logz = mx + log(reduce_sum(part_se, lid));
    float m = mask[row];
    float wt = weight[row];
    float gm = gmul * m * wt;
    uint tgt = target[row];
    for (uint i = lid; i < vocab; i += 256) {
        float logit = logits[base + i];
        float p = exp(logit - logz);
        if (i == tgt) {
            loss[row] = -m * wt * (logit - logz);
            logits[base + i] = gm * (p - 1.0);
        } else {
            logits[base + i] = gm * p;
        }
    }
}

void main() {
    uint row = gl_WorkGroupID.x;
    if (row < rows) {
        row_ce(row, gl_LocalInvocationID.x);
    }
}

/// file: shader/sumsq.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer buf_g {
    float g[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) buffer buf_partial {
    float partial[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) buffer buf_opt {
    float lr;
    float bc1;
    float bc2;
    float norm;
    float scale;
    float gmul;
    float good;
    float skip;
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint n;
    uint op;
    float gscale;
    float clip;
    float growth;
};

shared float red[256];

float reduce_sum(float v, uint lid) {
    red[lid] = v;
    barrier();
    for (uint s = 128; s > 0; s >>= 1) {
        if (lid < s) {
            red[lid] += red[lid + s];
        }
        barrier();
    }
    return red[0];
}

void main() {
    uint lid = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
    if (op == 0) {
        uint i = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
        float v = 0.0;
        if (i < n) {
            v = g[i];
        }
        float s = reduce_sum(v * v, lid);
        if (lid == 0) {
            partial[gl_WorkGroupID.x] = s;
        }
    } else {
        float acc = 0.0;
        for (uint i = lid; i < n; i += 256) {
            acc += partial[i];
        }
        float s = reduce_sum(acc, lid);
        if (lid == 0) {
            float nrm = gscale * sqrt(s) / gmul;
            norm = nrm;
            if (s <= 3.0e38) {
                skip = 0.0;
                scale = gscale / gmul * min(1.0, clip / (nrm + 1e-12));
                good = good + 1.0;
                if (good >= growth) {
                    gmul = gmul * 2.0;
                    good = 0.0;
                }
            } else {
                skip = 1.0;
                scale = 0.0;
                gmul = gmul * 0.5;
                good = 0.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

/// file: shader/temb.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer buf_w {
    float weight[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) writeonly buffer buf_e {
    float temb[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint batch;
    uint d;
    uint seqlen;
};

void main() {
    uint idx = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    if (idx < batch * d) {
        uint b = idx / d;
        uint i = idx % d;
        uint half_d = d / 2u;
        float t = 1.0 / weight[b * seqlen];
        float arg = 1000.0 * t * exp(-log(10000.0) * float(i % half_d) / float(half_d));
        if (i < half_d) {
            temb[idx] = sin(arg);
        } else {
            temb[idx] = cos(arg);
        }
    }
}

/// file: shader/tokenshift.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_EXT_shader_atomic_float : require
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer buf_x {
    float x[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer buf_mix {
    float mix[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) buffer buf_out {
    float outp[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 3) buffer buf_dx {
    float dx[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 4) buffer buf_dmix {
    float dmix[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint bt;
    uint d;
    uint seqlen;
    uint op;
};

float previous(uint row, uint col, uint t) {
    if (t == 0u) {
        return 0.0;
    } else {
        return x[(row - 1u) * d + col];
    }
}

void main() {
    uint idx = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    uint row = idx / d;
    uint col = idx % d;
    uint t = row % seqlen;
    if (idx < bt * d && op == 0) {
        float m = mix[col];
        outp[idx] = m * x[idx] + (1.0 - m) * previous(row, col, t);
    } else if (idx < bt * d) {
        float m = mix[col];
        float dout = outp[idx];
        atomicAdd(dmix[col], (x[idx] - previous(row, col, t)) * dout);
        atomicAdd(dx[idx], m * dout);
        if (t != 0u) {
            atomicAdd(dx[(row - 1u) * d + col], (1.0 - m) * dout);
        }
    }
}

/// file: shader/transpose_f16.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_EXT_shader_explicit_arithmetic_types_float16 : require
layout(local_size_x = 32, local_size_y = 8) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) readonly buffer buf_src {
    float src[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) writeonly buffer buf_dst {
    float16_t dst[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 2) readonly buffer buf_amax {
    uint amax[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint rows;
    uint cols;
    uint slot;
};

shared float tile[32][33];

float slot_scale(uint s) {
    if (s == ~0u) {
        return 1.0;
    } else if (!(uintBitsToFloat(amax[s]) > 0.0) || isinf(uintBitsToFloat(amax[s]))) {
        return 1.0;
    } else {
        return exp2(141.0 - float((amax[s] >> 23) & 0xffu));
    }
}

void main() {
    float sc = slot_scale(slot);
    uint c0 = gl_WorkGroupID.x * 32u;
    uint r0 = gl_WorkGroupID.y * 32u;
    uint tx = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
    uint ty = gl_LocalInvocationID.y;
    for (uint i = ty; i < 32u; i += 8u) {
        tile[i][tx] = src[(r0 + i) * cols + c0 + tx];
    }
    barrier();
    for (uint i = ty; i < 32u; i += 8u) {
        dst[(c0 + i) * rows + r0 + tx] = float16_t(tile[tx][i] * sc);
    }
}

/// file: shader/vecops.comp
#version 450
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

layout(std430, binding = 0) buffer buf_a {
    float a[];
};
layout(std430, binding = 1) readonly buffer buf_b {
    float b[];
};
layout(push_constant) uniform pc {
    uint n;
    uint op;
};

float combine(float x, float y) {
    if (op == 0) {
        return x + y;
    } else if (op == 1) {
        return x * y;
    } else {
        return 0.5 * (x + y);
    }
}

void main() {
    uint i = gl_GlobalInvocationID.x;
    if (i < n && op == 3) {
        a[i] = b[i];
    } else if (i < n) {
        a[i] = combine(a[i], b[i]);
    }
}

/// file: shader/wkv7_bwd.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_EXT_shader_explicit_arithmetic_types_float16 : require
#extension GL_KHR_shader_subgroup_arithmetic : require
#extension GL_EXT_control_flow_attributes : require
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

const uint CH = 4u;

layout(std430,binding=0) readonly  buffer BK  { float K[]; };
layout(std430,binding=1) readonly  buffer BV  { float V[]; };
layout(std430,binding=2) readonly  buffer BR  { float R[]; };
layout(std430,binding=3) readonly  buffer BW  { float W[]; };
layout(std430,binding=4) readonly  buffer BA  { float A[]; };
layout(std430,binding=5) readonly  buffer BSP { float16_t Sprev[]; };
layout(std430,binding=6) readonly  buffer BdO { float dO[]; };
layout(std430,binding=7) writeonly buffer BdK { float dK[]; };
layout(std430,binding=8) writeonly buffer BdV { float dV[]; };
layout(std430,binding=9) writeonly buffer BdR { float dR[]; };
layout(std430,binding=10) writeonly buffer BdW{ float dW[]; };
layout(std430,binding=11) writeonly buffer BdA{ float dA[]; };
layout(push_constant) uniform P { uint Bsz, Hd, T, DH, dir; };

shared float kk[64], vv[64], rr[64], ww[64], aa[64], dOs[64];
shared float p0[256], p1[256], p2[256];
shared float h0[128];
shared vec2 h1[128];

uint step_row(uint base, uint tf) {
    if (dir == 0u) {
        return base + tf*64u;
    } else {
        return base + (T - 1u - tf)*64u;
    }
}

void run(uint bh) {
    uint lid = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
    uint g = lid >> 6;
    uint e = lid & 63u;
    uint half_ = e >> 5;
    bool lead = (e & 31u) == 0u;
    uint base = bh*T*64u;
    uint nc = T / CH;
    uint cbase = bh*nc*4096u + lid;

    float Ps[CH][16];
    float dS[16];
    for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) dS[q] = 0.0;

    for (uint ci = 0; ci < nc; ci += 1u) {
        uint c = nc - 1u - ci;

        float S[16];
        for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) S[q] = float(Sprev[cbase + c*4096u + q*256u]);
        [[unroll]] for (uint j = 0; j < CH; j += 1u) {
            for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) Ps[j][q] = S[q];
            if (j + 1u < CH) {
                uint row = step_row(base, c*CH + j);
                if (lid < 64u) {
                    kk[lid] = K[row+lid]; vv[lid] = V[row+lid];
                    ww[lid] = W[row+lid]; aa[lid] = A[row+lid];
                }
                barrier();
                float s = 0.0;
                for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) s += S[q]*kk[g + 4u*q];
                p0[lid] = s;
                barrier();
                float sk = p0[e] + p0[64u+e] + p0[128u+e] + p0[192u+e];
                for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) {
                    uint i = g + 4u*q;
                    S[q] = ww[e]*S[q] + aa[i]*kk[i]*(vv[e] - sk);
                }
                barrier();
            }
        }

        [[unroll]] for (uint jj = 0; jj < CH; jj += 1u) {
            uint j = CH - 1u - jj;
            uint row = step_row(base, c*CH + j);
            if (lid < 64u) {
                kk[lid] = K[row+lid]; vv[lid] = V[row+lid]; rr[lid] = R[row+lid];
                ww[lid] = W[row+lid]; aa[lid] = A[row+lid]; dOs[lid] = dO[row+lid];
            }
            barrier();

            float s = 0.0;
            for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) s += Ps[j][q]*kk[g + 4u*q];
            p0[lid] = s;
            barrier();
            float sk = p0[e] + p0[64u+e] + p0[128u+e] + p0[192u+e];
            float we = ww[e];
            float ve = vv[e];
            float doe = dOs[e];

            float dw = 0.0;
            float dv = 0.0;
            for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) {
                uint i = g + 4u*q;
                float ak = aa[i]*kk[i];
                float x = subgroupAdd((we*Ps[j][q] + ak*(ve - sk))*doe);
                if (lead) h0[(g*16u + q)*2u + half_] = x;
                dS[q] += rr[i]*doe;
                dw += dS[q]*Ps[j][q];
                dv += dS[q]*ak;
            }
            p1[lid] = dw;
            p2[lid] = dv;
            barrier();
            float dve = p2[e] + p2[64u+e] + p2[128u+e] + p2[192u+e];
            float dsk = -dve;
            if (lid < 64u) {
                uint hi = ((lid & 3u)*16u + (lid >> 2))*2u;
                dR[row + lid] = h0[hi] + h0[hi + 1u];
                dW[row + lid] = p1[lid] + p1[64u+lid] + p1[128u+lid] + p1[192u+lid];
                dV[row + lid] = dve;
            }

            for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) {
                uint i = g + 4u*q;
                vec2 y = subgroupAdd(vec2(dS[q]*(ve - sk), dsk*Ps[j][q]));
                if (lead) h1[(g*16u + q)*2u + half_] = y;
                dS[q] = we*dS[q] + dsk*kk[i];
            }
            barrier();
            if (lid < 64u) {
                uint hi = ((lid & 3u)*16u + (lid >> 2))*2u;
                vec2 y = h1[hi] + h1[hi + 1u];
                dA[row + lid] = y.x*kk[lid];
                dK[row + lid] = y.x*aa[lid] + y.y;
            }
        }
    }
}

void main(){
    uint bh = gl_WorkGroupID.x;
    if (bh < Bsz*Hd) {
        run(bh);
    }
}

/// file: shader/wkv7_fwd.comp
#version 450
#extension GL_EXT_shader_explicit_arithmetic_types_float16 : require
layout(local_size_x = 256) in;

const uint CH = 4u;

layout(std430,binding=0) readonly  buffer BK { float K[]; };
layout(std430,binding=1) readonly  buffer BV { float V[]; };
layout(std430,binding=2) readonly  buffer BR { float R[]; };
layout(std430,binding=3) readonly  buffer BW { float W[]; };
layout(std430,binding=4) readonly  buffer BA { float A[]; };
layout(std430,binding=5) writeonly buffer BO { float O[]; };
layout(std430,binding=6) writeonly buffer BS { float16_t Sout[]; };

layout(push_constant) uniform P { uint Bsz, Hd, T, DH, dir, save; };

shared float kk[64], vv[64], rr[64], ww[64], aa[64];
shared float p0[256], p1[256];

uint step_t(uint ti) {
    if (dir == 0u) {
        return ti;
    } else {
        return T - 1u - ti;
    }
}

void run(uint bh) {
    uint lid = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
    uint g = lid >> 6;
    uint e = lid & 63u;
    uint base = bh*T*64u;

    float S[16];
    for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) S[q] = 0.0;

    for (uint ti = 0; ti < T; ti += 1u) {
        uint row = base + step_t(ti)*64u;
        if (lid < 64u) {
            kk[lid] = K[row+lid]; vv[lid] = V[row+lid];
            rr[lid] = R[row+lid]; ww[lid] = W[row+lid]; aa[lid] = A[row+lid];
        }
        barrier();
        if (save == 1u && (ti % CH) == 0u) {
            uint sb = bh*(T/CH)*4096u + (ti/CH)*4096u + lid;
            for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) Sout[sb + q*256u] = float16_t(S[q]);
        }
        float s = 0.0;
        for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) s += S[q]*kk[g + 4u*q];
        p0[lid] = s;
        barrier();
        float sk = p0[e] + p0[64u+e] + p0[128u+e] + p0[192u+e];
        float o = 0.0;
        for (uint q = 0; q < 16u; q += 1u) {
            uint i = g + 4u*q;
            S[q] = ww[e]*S[q] + aa[i]*kk[i]*(vv[e] - sk);
            o += rr[i]*S[q];
        }
        p1[lid] = o;
        barrier();
        if (lid < 64u) O[row+lid] = p1[lid] + p1[64u+lid] + p1[128u+lid] + p1[192u+lid];
    }
}

void main(){
    uint bh = gl_WorkGroupID.x;
    if (bh < Bsz*Hd) {
        run(bh);
    }
}

/// file: bench.c
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "bench.h"
#include "vkc.h"
#include "hash.h"
#include "util.h"
#include "sort.h"
#include "data.h"
#include "model.h"
#include "fwd.h"
#include "step.h"

void mda_bench_run(mda_step_trainer_t *tr, mda_bench_phase_t ph, uint32_t step) {
    switch (ph) {
    case MDA_BENCH_FORWARD:
        mda_fwd_run(tr->m, &tr->sc);
        break;
    case MDA_BENCH_GRAD:
        mda_step_grad(tr);
        break;
    case MDA_BENCH_STEP:
        mda_step_train(tr, 1e-5f, step);
        break;
    }
}

void mda_bench_phase(mda_step_trainer_t *tr, mda_bench_phase_t ph, const char *name) {
    mda_sort_item_t ms[MDA_BENCH_ROUNDS];
    uint32_t step = 1;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 2; i += 1) {
        mda_bench_run(tr, ph, step);
        step += 1;
    }
    for (uint32_t r = 0; r < MDA_BENCH_ROUNDS; r += 1) {
        double t0 = mda_util_now();
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MDA_BENCH_ITERS; i += 1) {
            mda_bench_run(tr, ph, step);
            step += 1;
        }
        ms[r] = (mda_sort_item_t) {
            .key = (mda_util_now() - t0) / MDA_BENCH_ITERS * 1e3,
            .idx = r,
        };
    }
    mda_sort_items(ms, MDA_BENCH_ROUNDS);
    uint32_t tokens = tr->m->cfg.batch * tr->m->cfg.seqlen;
    printf("%-8s %10.2f %10.2f %12.0f\n", name, ms[0].key, ms[MDA_BENCH_ROUNDS / 2].key, tokens / (ms[0].key / 1e3));
}

void mda_bench_profile(mda_step_trainer_t *tr) {
    mda_vkc_t *c = tr->m->c;
    mda_vkc_prof_begin(c);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MDA_BENCH_PROF_BATCHES; i += 1) {
        mda_step_grad(tr);
        mda_vkc_begin(c);
        mda_step_opt_rec(tr, 1.0f);
        mda_vkc_flush(c);
    }
    if (c->fail == false) {
        printf("GPU time per training step:\n");
        mda_vkc_prof_report(c, MDA_BENCH_PROF_BATCHES);
    }
}

bool mda_bench_trainer(mda_step_trainer_t *tr) {
    uint32_t *cids = malloc(MDA_BENCH_CORPUS_LEN * sizeof(uint32_t));
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MDA_BENCH_CORPUS_LEN; i += 1) {
        cids[i] = (uint32_t)(mda_hash_u64(MDA_HASH_CORPUS, 0, i) % MDA_BENCH_VOCAB);
    }
    mda_data_corpus_t cp = {
        .ids = cids,
        .len = MDA_BENCH_CORPUS_LEN,
        .eval_start = MDA_BENCH_CORPUS_LEN,
        .mask_id = MDA_BENCH_VOCAB,
    };
    mda_data_batch(&tr->sc.batch, &cp, MDA_DATA_SPLIT_TRAIN, 0);
    printf("%-8s %10s %10s %12s\n", "phase", "min ms", "median ms", "tok/s");
    mda_bench_phase(tr, MDA_BENCH_FORWARD, "forward");
    mda_bench_phase(tr, MDA_BENCH_GRAD, "fwd+bwd");
    mda_bench_phase(tr, MDA_BENCH_STEP, "step");
    if (tr->m->c->fail == false) {
        mda_bench_profile(tr);
    }
    free(cids);
    return tr->m->c->fail == false;
}

bool mda_bench_device(mda_vkc_t *c, bool muon) {
    mda_model_t *m = mda_model_init(c, mda_model_cfg(MDA_BENCH_VOCAB));
    if (m == NULL) {
        return false;
    } else {
        mda_step_trainer_t tr = mda_step_init(m, 1.0f, 1, muon);
        if (c->fail) {
            return false;
        } else {
            return mda_bench_trainer(&tr);
        }
    }
}

bool mda_bench_main(uint32_t argc, char **argv) {
    bool muon = strcmp(mda_util_arg(argc, argv, 1, "adamw"), "muon") == 0;
    mda_vkc_t c = mda_vkc_init();
    if (c.fail) {
        return false;
    } else {
        return mda_bench_device(&c, muon);
    }
}

int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    bool ok = mda_bench_main((uint32_t)argc, argv);
    if (ok) {
        return 0;
    } else {
        return 1;
    }
}

/// file: bench.h
#ifndef MDA_BENCH_H
#define MDA_BENCH_H

#include <stdint.h>

#define MDA_BENCH_CORPUS_LEN (1u << 20)
#define MDA_BENCH_ITERS 5
#define MDA_BENCH_PROF_BATCHES 3
#define MDA_BENCH_ROUNDS 7
#define MDA_BENCH_VOCAB 16000

typedef uint32_t mda_bench_phase_t;

enum {
    MDA_BENCH_FORWARD,
    MDA_BENCH_GRAD,
    MDA_BENCH_STEP,
};

#endif

/// file: bwd.c
#include "bwd.h"
#include "pc.h"

void mda_bwd_mm_da(mda_model_t *m, mda_vkc_buf_t *b, mda_vkc_buf_t *dc, mda_vkc_buf_t *da, uint32_t rows, uint32_t n, uint32_t k) {
    mda_pc_mm_bw_t pc = {
        .m = rows,
        .n = n,
        .k = k,
        .mode = 0,
    };
    mda_vkc_buf_t *bufs[4] = {
        b,
        b,
        dc,
        da,
    };
    mda_vkc_run(m->c, &m->k_mm_bw, bufs, (k + 15) / 16, (rows + 15) / 16, 1, &pc);
}

void mda_bwd_mm_db(mda_model_t *m, mda_vkc_buf_t *a, mda_vkc_buf_t *dc, mda_vkc_buf_t *db, uint32_t rows, uint32_t n, uint32_t k) {
    mda_pc_mm_bw_t pc = {
        .m = rows,
        .n = n,
        .k = k,
        .mode = 1,
    };
    mda_vkc_buf_t *bufs[4] = {
        a,
        a,
        dc,
        db,
    };
    mda_vkc_run(m->c, &m->k_mm_bw, bufs, (n + 15) / 16, (k + 15) / 16, 1, &pc);
}

void mda_bwd_mm_at(mda_model_t *m, mda_fwd_scratch_t *sc, mda_vkc_buf_t *a, uint32_t rows, uint32_t k) {
    if (m->ref32 == false) {
        sc->at16_slot = mda_fwd_absmax(m, a, rows * k);
        mda_fwd_transpose16(m, a, &sc->at16, rows, k, sc->at16_slot);
    }
}

void mda_bwd_mm(mda_model_t *m, mda_fwd_scratch_t *sc, mda_vkc_buf_t *a, mda_model_param_t *p, mda_vkc_buf_t *dout, mda_vkc_buf_t *da, uint32_t rows, uint32_t n, uint32_t k) {
    if (m->ref32) {
        mda_bwd_mm_db(m, a, dout, &p->g, rows, n, k);
        mda_bwd_mm_da(m, &p->w, dout, da, rows, n, k);
    } else {
        uint32_t sd = mda_fwd_cast_act(m, dout, &sc->dc16, rows * n);
        mda_vkc_set_nobar(m->c, true);
        mda_fwd_mm16_raw(m, &sc->at16, sc->at16_slot, &sc->dc16, sd, &p->g, k, n, rows, m->grad_acc);
        mda_vkc_set_nobar(m->c, false);
        mda_fwd_mm16_raw(m, &sc->dc16, sd, &p->wt16, MDA_PC_NO_SLOT, da, rows, k, n, false);
    }
}
