/// file: vkc.c
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "vkc.h"
#include "util.h"
#include "sort.h"

const char *const mda_vkc_dev_ext[2] = {
    VK_EXT_SHADER_ATOMIC_FLOAT_EXTENSION_NAME,
    VK_KHR_COOPERATIVE_MATRIX_EXTENSION_NAME,
};

void mda_vkc_check(mda_vkc_t *c, VkResult r, const char *file, uint32_t line) {
    if (r != VK_SUCCESS) {
        fprintf(stderr, "vk err %d %s:%u\n", r, file, line);
        c->fail = true;
    }
}

int32_t mda_vkc_memtype(VkPhysicalDevice pd, VkMemoryPropertyFlags want) {
    VkPhysicalDeviceMemoryProperties mp;
    vkGetPhysicalDeviceMemoryProperties(pd, &mp);
    uint32_t i = 0;
    while (i < mp.memoryTypeCount && (mp.memoryTypes[i].propertyFlags & want) != want) {
        i += 1;
    }
    if (i == mp.memoryTypeCount) {
        return -1;
    } else {
        return (int32_t)i;
    }
}

bool mda_vkc_has_ext(VkPhysicalDevice pd, const char *name) {
    uint32_t n = 0;
    vkEnumerateDeviceExtensionProperties(pd, 0, &n, 0);
    VkExtensionProperties *e = malloc(n * sizeof(VkExtensionProperties));
    vkEnumerateDeviceExtensionProperties(pd, 0, &n, e);
    uint32_t i = 0;
    while (i < n && strcmp(e[i].extensionName, name) != 0) {
        i += 1;
    }
    free(e);
    return i < n;
}

bool mda_vkc_has_tile(VkInstance inst, VkPhysicalDevice pd) {
    PFN_vkGetPhysicalDeviceCooperativeMatrixPropertiesKHR get_props = (PFN_vkGetPhysicalDeviceCooperativeMatrixPropertiesKHR)vkGetInstanceProcAddr(inst, "vkGetPhysicalDeviceCooperativeMatrixPropertiesKHR");
    uint32_t n = 0;
    get_props(pd, &n, 0);
    VkCooperativeMatrixPropertiesKHR *cp = calloc(n, sizeof(VkCooperativeMatrixPropertiesKHR));
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; i += 1) {
        cp[i].sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_COOPERATIVE_MATRIX_PROPERTIES_KHR;
    }
    get_props(pd, &n, cp);
    uint32_t i = 0;
    while (i < n && (cp[i].MSize != 16 || cp[i].NSize != 16 || cp[i].KSize != 16 || cp[i].AType != VK_COMPONENT_TYPE_FLOAT16_KHR || cp[i].BType != VK_COMPONENT_TYPE_FLOAT16_KHR || cp[i].CType != VK_COMPONENT_TYPE_FLOAT32_KHR || cp[i].ResultType != VK_COMPONENT_TYPE_FLOAT32_KHR || cp[i].scope != VK_SCOPE_SUBGROUP_KHR)) {
        i += 1;
    }
    free(cp);
    return i < n;
}

uint32_t mda_vkc_compute_family(VkPhysicalDevice pd) {
    uint32_t n = 0;
    vkGetPhysicalDeviceQueueFamilyProperties(pd, &n, 0);
    VkQueueFamilyProperties *qf = malloc(n * sizeof(VkQueueFamilyProperties));
    vkGetPhysicalDeviceQueueFamilyProperties(pd, &n, qf);
    uint32_t i = 0;
    while (i < n && (qf[i].queueFlags & VK_QUEUE_COMPUTE_BIT) == 0) {
        i += 1;
    }
    free(qf);
    if (i == n) {
        return MDA_VKC_NO_FAMILY;
    } else {
        return i;
    }
}

const char *mda_vkc_missing_ext(VkPhysicalDevice pd) {
    uint32_t i = 0;
    while (i < 2 && mda_vkc_has_ext(pd, mda_vkc_dev_ext[i])) {
        i += 1;
    }
    if (i < 2) {
        return mda_vkc_dev_ext[i];
    } else {
        return NULL;
    }
}

const char *mda_vkc_missing_feature(VkInstance inst, VkPhysicalDevice pd) {
    VkPhysicalDeviceVulkan11Properties p11 = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_VULKAN_1_1_PROPERTIES,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceVulkan13Properties p13 = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_VULKAN_1_3_PROPERTIES,
        .pNext = &p11,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceProperties2 pr = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_PROPERTIES_2,
        .pNext = &p13,
    };
    vkGetPhysicalDeviceProperties2(pd, &pr);
    VkPhysicalDeviceCooperativeMatrixFeaturesKHR coopmat = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_COOPERATIVE_MATRIX_FEATURES_KHR,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceShaderAtomicFloatFeaturesEXT atomic_float = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_SHADER_ATOMIC_FLOAT_FEATURES_EXT,
        .pNext = &coopmat,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceVulkan11Features f11 = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_VULKAN_1_1_FEATURES,
        .pNext = &atomic_float,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceVulkan12Features f12 = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_VULKAN_1_2_FEATURES,
        .pNext = &f11,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceVulkan13Features f13 = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_VULKAN_1_3_FEATURES,
        .pNext = &f12,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceFeatures2 f = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_FEATURES_2,
        .pNext = &f13,
    };
    vkGetPhysicalDeviceFeatures2(pd, &f);
    if (coopmat.cooperativeMatrix == 0 || atomic_float.shaderBufferFloat32AtomicAdd == 0 || f11.storageBuffer16BitAccess == 0 || f12.shaderFloat16 == 0 || f13.subgroupSizeControl == 0) {
        return "missing a required feature";
    } else if (p13.minSubgroupSize > 32 || p13.maxSubgroupSize < 32 || (p13.requiredSubgroupSizeStages & VK_SHADER_STAGE_COMPUTE_BIT) == 0) {
        return "no wave32 compute";
    } else if ((p11.subgroupSupportedOperations & VK_SUBGROUP_FEATURE_ARITHMETIC_BIT) == 0 || (p11.subgroupSupportedStages & VK_SHADER_STAGE_COMPUTE_BIT) == 0) {
        return "no subgroup arithmetic in compute";
    } else if (mda_vkc_has_tile(inst, pd) == false) {
        return "no 16x16x16 f16 cooperative matrix";
    } else if (mda_vkc_compute_family(pd) == MDA_VKC_NO_FAMILY) {
        return "no compute queue";
    } else if (mda_vkc_memtype(pd, VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_DEVICE_LOCAL_BIT | VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_VISIBLE_BIT | VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_COHERENT_BIT) < 0 || mda_vkc_memtype(pd, VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_VISIBLE_BIT | VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_COHERENT_BIT | VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_CACHED_BIT) < 0) {
        return "no host-visible device-local or host-cached memory";
    } else {
        return NULL;
    }
}

const char *mda_vkc_unsuitable(VkInstance inst, VkPhysicalDevice pd) {
    VkPhysicalDeviceProperties pr;
    vkGetPhysicalDeviceProperties(pd, &pr);
    if (pr.deviceType == VK_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_TYPE_CPU) {
        return "a CPU device";
    } else if (pr.apiVersion < VK_API_VERSION_1_3) {
        return "no Vulkan 1.3";
    } else if (mda_vkc_missing_ext(pd) != NULL) {
        return mda_vkc_missing_ext(pd);
    } else {
        return mda_vkc_missing_feature(inst, pd);
    }
}

int32_t mda_vkc_rank(VkPhysicalDeviceType t) {
    switch (t) {
    case VK_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_TYPE_DISCRETE_GPU:
        return 2;
    case VK_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_TYPE_INTEGRATED_GPU:
        return 1;
    default:
        return 0;
    }
}

VkPhysicalDevice mda_vkc_pick(VkInstance inst) {
    uint32_t n = 0;
    vkEnumeratePhysicalDevices(inst, &n, 0);
    VkPhysicalDevice *pds = malloc(n * sizeof(VkPhysicalDevice));
    vkEnumeratePhysicalDevices(inst, &n, pds);
    VkPhysicalDevice best = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    int32_t best_rank = -1;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; i += 1) {
        VkPhysicalDeviceProperties pr;
        vkGetPhysicalDeviceProperties(pds[i], &pr);
        const char *why = mda_vkc_unsuitable(inst, pds[i]);
        if (why != NULL) {
            fprintf(stderr, "vkc: skipping %s: %s\n", pr.deviceName, why);
        } else if (mda_vkc_rank(pr.deviceType) > best_rank) {
            best_rank = mda_vkc_rank(pr.deviceType);
            best = pds[i];
        }
    }
    free(pds);
    return best;
}

mda_vkc_t mda_vkc_init_pools(mda_vkc_t c) {
    vkGetDeviceQueue(c.dev, c.qfi, 0, &c.q);
    VkCommandPoolCreateInfo cpi = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_COMMAND_POOL_CREATE_INFO,
        .flags = VK_COMMAND_POOL_CREATE_RESET_COMMAND_BUFFER_BIT,
        .queueFamilyIndex = c.qfi,
    };
    MDA_VKC_CK(&c, vkCreateCommandPool(c.dev, &cpi, 0, &c.cpool));
    VkDescriptorPoolSize ps = {
        .type = VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_STORAGE_BUFFER,
        .descriptorCount = 32768,
    };
    VkDescriptorPoolCreateInfo dpi = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_POOL_CREATE_INFO,
        .flags = VK_DESCRIPTOR_POOL_CREATE_FREE_DESCRIPTOR_SET_BIT,
        .maxSets = 8192,
        .poolSizeCount = 1,
        .pPoolSizes = &ps,
    };
    MDA_VKC_CK(&c, vkCreateDescriptorPool(c.dev, &dpi, 0, &c.dpool));
    VkQueryPoolCreateInfo qpi = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_QUERY_POOL_CREATE_INFO,
        .queryType = VK_QUERY_TYPE_TIMESTAMP,
        .queryCount = MDA_VKC_MAX_QUERIES,
    };
    MDA_VKC_CK(&c, vkCreateQueryPool(c.dev, &qpi, 0, &c.qpool));
    c.qname = malloc(MDA_VKC_MAX_QUERIES * sizeof(const char *));
    return c;
}

mda_vkc_t mda_vkc_init_device(mda_vkc_t c) {
    VkPhysicalDeviceProperties pr;
    vkGetPhysicalDeviceProperties(c.pd, &pr);
    fprintf(stderr, "vkc GPU: %s\n", pr.deviceName);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) {
        c.max_groups[i] = pr.limits.maxComputeWorkGroupCount[i];
    }
    c.ts_period = pr.limits.timestampPeriod;
    c.mem_dev = (uint32_t)mda_vkc_memtype(c.pd, VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_DEVICE_LOCAL_BIT | VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_VISIBLE_BIT | VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_COHERENT_BIT);
    c.mem_cached = (uint32_t)mda_vkc_memtype(c.pd, VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_VISIBLE_BIT | VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_COHERENT_BIT | VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_CACHED_BIT);
    c.qfi = mda_vkc_compute_family(c.pd);
    float pri = 1.0f;
    VkDeviceQueueCreateInfo qci = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DEVICE_QUEUE_CREATE_INFO,
        .queueFamilyIndex = c.qfi,
        .queueCount = 1,
        .pQueuePriorities = &pri,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceCooperativeMatrixFeaturesKHR coopmat = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_COOPERATIVE_MATRIX_FEATURES_KHR,
        .cooperativeMatrix = VK_TRUE,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceShaderAtomicFloatFeaturesEXT atomic_float = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_SHADER_ATOMIC_FLOAT_FEATURES_EXT,
        .pNext = &coopmat,
        .shaderBufferFloat32AtomicAdd = VK_TRUE,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceVulkan11Features f11 = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_VULKAN_1_1_FEATURES,
        .pNext = &atomic_float,
        .storageBuffer16BitAccess = VK_TRUE,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceVulkan12Features f12 = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_VULKAN_1_2_FEATURES,
        .pNext = &f11,
        .shaderFloat16 = VK_TRUE,
    };
    VkPhysicalDeviceVulkan13Features f13 = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_VULKAN_1_3_FEATURES,
        .pNext = &f12,
        .subgroupSizeControl = VK_TRUE,
    };
    VkDeviceCreateInfo dci = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DEVICE_CREATE_INFO,
        .pNext = &f13,
        .queueCreateInfoCount = 1,
        .pQueueCreateInfos = &qci,
        .enabledExtensionCount = 2,
        .ppEnabledExtensionNames = mda_vkc_dev_ext,
    };
    MDA_VKC_CK(&c, vkCreateDevice(c.pd, &dci, 0, &c.dev));
    if (c.fail) {
        return c;
    } else {
        return mda_vkc_init_pools(c);
    }
}

mda_vkc_t mda_vkc_init_instance(mda_vkc_t c) {
    c.pd = mda_vkc_pick(c.inst);
    if (c.pd == VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
        fprintf(stderr, "vkc: no suitable Vulkan device\n");
        c.fail = true;
        return c;
    } else {
        return mda_vkc_init_device(c);
    }
}

mda_vkc_t mda_vkc_init(void) {
    mda_vkc_t c = {
        .inst = VK_NULL_HANDLE,
    };
    VkApplicationInfo app = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_APPLICATION_INFO,
        .apiVersion = VK_API_VERSION_1_3,
    };
    VkInstanceCreateInfo ici = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_INSTANCE_CREATE_INFO,
        .pApplicationInfo = &app,
    };
    MDA_VKC_CK(&c, vkCreateInstance(&ici, 0, &c.inst));
    if (c.fail) {
        return c;
    } else {
        return mda_vkc_init_instance(c);
    }
}

mda_vkc_buf_t mda_vkc_bind_memory(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_buf_t b, uint32_t type) {
    MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkBindBufferMemory(c->dev, b.buf, b.mem, 0));
    VkPhysicalDeviceMemoryProperties mp;
    vkGetPhysicalDeviceMemoryProperties(c->pd, &mp);
    if (c->fail == false && (mp.memoryTypes[type].propertyFlags & VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_VISIBLE_BIT) != 0) {
        MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkMapMemory(c->dev, b.mem, 0, b.sz, 0, &b.ptr));
    }
    return b;
}

mda_vkc_buf_t mda_vkc_alloc_memory(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_buf_t b, uint32_t type) {
    VkMemoryRequirements mr;
    vkGetBufferMemoryRequirements(c->dev, b.buf, &mr);
    if ((mr.memoryTypeBits & (1u << type)) == 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "memory type %u can't back a storage buffer\n", type);
        c->fail = true;
        return b;
    } else {
        VkMemoryAllocateInfo ai = {
            .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_MEMORY_ALLOCATE_INFO,
            .allocationSize = mr.size,
            .memoryTypeIndex = type,
        };
        MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkAllocateMemory(c->dev, &ai, 0, &b.mem));
        return b;
    }
}

mda_vkc_buf_t mda_vkc_alloc_type(mda_vkc_t *c, VkDeviceSize sz, uint32_t type) {
    mda_vkc_buf_t b = {
        .sz = sz,
    };
    VkBufferCreateInfo bi = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_BUFFER_CREATE_INFO,
        .size = sz,
        .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT | VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT | VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT,
        .sharingMode = VK_SHARING_MODE_EXCLUSIVE,
    };
    if (c->fail == false) {
        MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkCreateBuffer(c->dev, &bi, 0, &b.buf));
    }
    if (c->fail == false) {
        b = mda_vkc_alloc_memory(c, b, type);
    }
    if (c->fail == false) {
        b = mda_vkc_bind_memory(c, b, type);
    }
    return b;
}

mda_vkc_buf_t mda_vkc_alloc(mda_vkc_t *c, VkDeviceSize sz) {
    return mda_vkc_alloc_type(c, sz, c->mem_dev);
}

mda_vkc_buf_t mda_vkc_alloc_cached(mda_vkc_t *c, VkDeviceSize sz) {
    return mda_vkc_alloc_type(c, sz, c->mem_cached);
}

void mda_vkc_free(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_buf_t *b) {
    vkDestroyBuffer(c->dev, b->buf, 0);
    vkFreeMemory(c->dev, b->mem, 0);
}

void *mda_vkc_offset(void *ptr, VkDeviceSize off) {
    if (ptr == NULL) {
        return NULL;
    } else {
        return (char *)ptr + off;
    }
}

mda_vkc_buf_t mda_vkc_view(mda_vkc_buf_t *b, VkDeviceSize off, VkDeviceSize sz) {
    return (mda_vkc_buf_t) {
        .buf = b->buf,
        .mem = b->mem,
        .ptr = mda_vkc_offset(b->ptr, off),
        .off = b->off + off,
        .sz = sz,
    };
}

mda_vkc_spv_t mda_vkc_spv_none(void) {
    return (mda_vkc_spv_t) {
        .b = NULL,
        .len = 0,
    };
}

mda_vkc_spv_t mda_vkc_read_file(FILE *f, const char *p) {
    fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END);
    int64_t n = ftell(f);
    fseek(f, 0, SEEK_SET);
    uint32_t *b = malloc(n);
    size_t got = fread(b, 1, n, f);
    fclose(f);
    if (got != (size_t)n) {
        fprintf(stderr, "%s: short read\n", p);
        free(b);
        return mda_vkc_spv_none();
    } else {
        return (mda_vkc_spv_t) {
            .b = b,
            .len = (size_t)n,
        };
    }
}

mda_vkc_spv_t mda_vkc_read_spv(const char *p) {
    FILE *f = fopen(p, "rb");
    if (f == NULL) {
        perror(p);
        return mda_vkc_spv_none();
    } else {
        return mda_vkc_read_file(f, p);
    }
}

mda_vkc_kernel_t mda_vkc_load_pipeline(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_kernel_t k, VkShaderModule sm) {
    VkDescriptorSetLayoutBinding *b = calloc(k.nbuf, sizeof(VkDescriptorSetLayoutBinding));
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < k.nbuf; i += 1) {
        b[i] = (VkDescriptorSetLayoutBinding) {
            .binding = i,
            .descriptorType = VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_STORAGE_BUFFER,
            .descriptorCount = 1,
            .stageFlags = VK_SHADER_STAGE_COMPUTE_BIT,
        };
    }
    VkDescriptorSetLayoutCreateInfo dli = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_SET_LAYOUT_CREATE_INFO,
        .bindingCount = k.nbuf,
        .pBindings = b,
    };
    MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkCreateDescriptorSetLayout(c->dev, &dli, 0, &k.dsl));
    free(b);
    VkPushConstantRange pcr = {
        .stageFlags = VK_SHADER_STAGE_COMPUTE_BIT,
        .offset = 0,
        .size = mda_util_max_u32(k.push_sz, 4),
    };
    VkPipelineLayoutCreateInfo pli = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PIPELINE_LAYOUT_CREATE_INFO,
        .setLayoutCount = 1,
        .pSetLayouts = &k.dsl,
        .pushConstantRangeCount = 1,
        .pPushConstantRanges = &pcr,
    };
    MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkCreatePipelineLayout(c->dev, &pli, 0, &k.pl));
    VkPipelineShaderStageRequiredSubgroupSizeCreateInfo rss = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PIPELINE_SHADER_STAGE_REQUIRED_SUBGROUP_SIZE_CREATE_INFO,
        .requiredSubgroupSize = 32,
    };
    VkComputePipelineCreateInfo cpi = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_COMPUTE_PIPELINE_CREATE_INFO,
        .stage = (VkPipelineShaderStageCreateInfo) {
            .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PIPELINE_SHADER_STAGE_CREATE_INFO,
            .pNext = &rss,
            .stage = VK_SHADER_STAGE_COMPUTE_BIT,
            .module = sm,
            .pName = "main",
        },
        .layout = k.pl,
    };
    MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkCreateComputePipelines(c->dev, 0, 1, &cpi, 0, &k.pipe));
    vkDestroyShaderModule(c->dev, sm, 0);
    return k;
}

mda_vkc_kernel_t mda_vkc_load_module(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_kernel_t k, mda_vkc_spv_t sp) {
    VkShaderModuleCreateInfo smi = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_SHADER_MODULE_CREATE_INFO,
        .codeSize = sp.len,
        .pCode = sp.b,
    };
    VkShaderModule sm = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkCreateShaderModule(c->dev, &smi, 0, &sm));
    free(sp.b);
    if (c->fail) {
        return k;
    } else {
        return mda_vkc_load_pipeline(c, k, sm);
    }
}

mda_vkc_kernel_t mda_vkc_load_spv(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_kernel_t k) {
    mda_vkc_spv_t sp = mda_vkc_read_spv(k.name);
    if (sp.b == NULL) {
        c->fail = true;
        return k;
    } else {
        return mda_vkc_load_module(c, k, sp);
    }
}

mda_vkc_kernel_t mda_vkc_load(mda_vkc_t *c, const char *spv, uint32_t nbuf, uint32_t push_sz) {
    mda_vkc_kernel_t k = {
        .name = spv,
        .nbuf = nbuf,
        .push_sz = push_sz,
    };
    if (c->fail) {
        return k;
    } else if (nbuf > MDA_VKC_MAX_BINDINGS) {
        fprintf(stderr, "%s: %u bindings, at most %u supported\n", spv, nbuf, MDA_VKC_MAX_BINDINGS);
        c->fail = true;
        return k;
    } else {
        return mda_vkc_load_spv(c, k);
    }
}

void mda_vkc_barrier(VkCommandBuffer cb) {
    VkMemoryBarrier mb = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_MEMORY_BARRIER,
        .srcAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_SHADER_WRITE_BIT | VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT,
        .dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_SHADER_READ_BIT | VK_ACCESS_SHADER_WRITE_BIT | VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_READ_BIT | VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT,
    };
    VkPipelineStageFlags st = VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT | VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT;
    vkCmdPipelineBarrier(cb, st, st, 0, 1, &mb, 0, 0, 0, 0);
}

void mda_vkc_host_barrier(VkCommandBuffer cb) {
    VkMemoryBarrier mb = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_MEMORY_BARRIER,
        .srcAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_SHADER_WRITE_BIT | VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT,
        .dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_HOST_READ_BIT,
    };
    vkCmdPipelineBarrier(cb, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT | VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_HOST_BIT, 0, 1, &mb, 0, 0, 0, 0);
}

VkCommandBuffer mda_vkc_cb_begin(mda_vkc_t *c, VkCommandBufferUsageFlags flags) {
    VkCommandBufferAllocateInfo cbi = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_COMMAND_BUFFER_ALLOCATE_INFO,
        .commandPool = c->cpool,
        .level = VK_COMMAND_BUFFER_LEVEL_PRIMARY,
        .commandBufferCount = 1,
    };
    VkCommandBufferBeginInfo bb = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_COMMAND_BUFFER_BEGIN_INFO,
        .flags = flags,
    };
    VkCommandBuffer cb = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkAllocateCommandBuffers(c->dev, &cbi, &cb));
    if (c->fail == false) {
        MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkBeginCommandBuffer(cb, &bb));
    }
    return cb;
}

void mda_vkc_cb_submit(mda_vkc_t *c, VkCommandBuffer cb) {
    VkSubmitInfo si = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_SUBMIT_INFO,
        .commandBufferCount = 1,
        .pCommandBuffers = &cb,
    };
    if (c->fail == false) {
        MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkQueueSubmit(c->q, 1, &si, 0));
    }
    if (c->fail == false) {
        MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkQueueWaitIdle(c->q));
    }
}

void mda_vkc_begin(mda_vkc_t *c) {
    if (c->fail == false) {
        c->nobar = false;
        c->rec = mda_vkc_cb_begin(c, VK_COMMAND_BUFFER_USAGE_ONE_TIME_SUBMIT_BIT);
        c->rec_pool = c->dpool;
        c->recording = true;
        c->prof_rec = c->prof;
    }
    if (c->fail == false && c->prof_rec) {
        vkCmdResetQueryPool(c->rec, c->qpool, 0, MDA_VKC_MAX_QUERIES);
        vkCmdWriteTimestamp(c->rec, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_BOTTOM_OF_PIPE_BIT, c->qpool, 0);
        c->nq = 1;
    }
}

void mda_vkc_prof_mark(mda_vkc_t *c, const char *name) {
    if (c->prof_rec && c->nq < MDA_VKC_MAX_QUERIES) {
        vkCmdWriteTimestamp(c->rec, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_BOTTOM_OF_PIPE_BIT, c->qpool, c->nq);
        c->qname[c->nq] = name;
        c->nq += 1;
    }
}

uint32_t mda_vkc_prof_label(mda_vkc_t *c, const char *name) {
    uint32_t i = 0;
    while (i < c->prof_n && c->prof_name[i] != name) {
        i += 1;
    }
    if (i == c->prof_n && i < MDA_VKC_MAX_LABELS) {
        c->prof_name[i] = name;
        c->prof_ms[i] = 0.0;
        c->prof_count[i] = 0;
        c->prof_n += 1;
    }
    return i;
}

void mda_vkc_prof_collect(mda_vkc_t *c) {
    uint64_t *ts = malloc(c->nq * sizeof(uint64_t));
    MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkGetQueryPoolResults(c->dev, c->qpool, 0, c->nq, c->nq * sizeof(uint64_t), ts, sizeof(uint64_t), VK_QUERY_RESULT_64_BIT | VK_QUERY_RESULT_WAIT_BIT));
    for (uint32_t q = 1; q < c->nq && c->fail == false; q += 1) {
        uint32_t i = mda_vkc_prof_label(c, c->qname[q]);
        if (i < MDA_VKC_MAX_LABELS) {
            c->prof_ms[i] += (double)(ts[q] - ts[q - 1]) * c->ts_period / 1e6;
            c->prof_count[i] += 1;
        }
    }
    free(ts);
}

void mda_vkc_prof_begin(mda_vkc_t *c) {
    c->prof = true;
    c->prof_n = 0;
}

void mda_vkc_prof_report(mda_vkc_t *c, uint32_t batches) {
    c->prof = false;
    double total = 0.0;
    mda_sort_item_t order[MDA_VKC_MAX_LABELS];
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < c->prof_n; i += 1) {
        total += c->prof_ms[i];
        order[i] = (mda_sort_item_t) {
            .key = -c->prof_ms[i],
            .idx = i,
        };
    }
    mda_sort_items(order, c->prof_n);
    printf("%-28s %10s %7s %10s\n", "kernel", "ms/batch", "share", "per batch");
    for (uint32_t r = 0; r < c->prof_n; r += 1) {
        uint32_t i = order[r].idx;
        printf("%-28s %10.3f %6.1f%% %10u\n", c->prof_name[i], c->prof_ms[i] / batches, 100.0 * c->prof_ms[i] / total, c->prof_count[i] / batches);
    }
    printf("%-28s %10.3f\n", "total", total / batches);
}

void mda_vkc_flush(mda_vkc_t *c) {
    if (c->fail == false) {
        mda_vkc_host_barrier(c->rec);
        MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkEndCommandBuffer(c->rec));
        mda_vkc_cb_submit(c, c->rec);
        vkFreeCommandBuffers(c->dev, c->cpool, 1, &c->rec);
        MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkResetDescriptorPool(c->dev, c->dpool, 0));
        c->recording = false;
    }
    if (c->fail == false && c->prof_rec) {
        mda_vkc_prof_collect(c);
        c->prof_rec = false;
    }
}

mda_vkc_cmd_t mda_vkc_record(mda_vkc_t *c) {
    mda_vkc_cmd_t cmd = {
        .cb = VK_NULL_HANDLE,
        .pool = VK_NULL_HANDLE,
    };
    VkDescriptorPoolSize ps = {
        .type = VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_STORAGE_BUFFER,
        .descriptorCount = 32768,
    };
    VkDescriptorPoolCreateInfo dpi = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_POOL_CREATE_INFO,
        .maxSets = 8192,
        .poolSizeCount = 1,
        .pPoolSizes = &ps,
    };
    if (c->fail == false) {
        MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkCreateDescriptorPool(c->dev, &dpi, 0, &cmd.pool));
    }
    if (c->fail == false) {
        c->nobar = false;
        c->rec = mda_vkc_cb_begin(c, 0);
        c->rec_pool = cmd.pool;
        c->recording = true;
        c->prof_rec = false;
        cmd.cb = c->rec;
    }
    return cmd;
}

void mda_vkc_end(mda_vkc_t *c) {
    if (c->fail == false) {
        mda_vkc_host_barrier(c->rec);
        MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkEndCommandBuffer(c->rec));
        c->recording = false;
    }
}

void mda_vkc_submit(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_cmd_t *cmd) {
    mda_vkc_cb_submit(c, cmd->cb);
}

void mda_vkc_bind_set(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_kernel_t *k, mda_vkc_buf_t **bufs, VkDescriptorSet ds) {
    VkWriteDescriptorSet w[MDA_VKC_MAX_BINDINGS];
    VkDescriptorBufferInfo bi[MDA_VKC_MAX_BINDINGS];
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < k->nbuf; i += 1) {
        bi[i] = (VkDescriptorBufferInfo) {
            .buffer = bufs[i]->buf,
            .offset = bufs[i]->off,
            .range = bufs[i]->sz,
        };
        w[i] = (VkWriteDescriptorSet) {
            .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_WRITE_DESCRIPTOR_SET,
            .dstSet = ds,
            .dstBinding = i,
            .descriptorCount = 1,
            .descriptorType = VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_STORAGE_BUFFER,
            .pBufferInfo = &bi[i],
        };
    }
    vkUpdateDescriptorSets(c->dev, k->nbuf, w, 0, 0);
    vkCmdBindPipeline(c->rec, VK_PIPELINE_BIND_POINT_COMPUTE, k->pipe);
    vkCmdBindDescriptorSets(c->rec, VK_PIPELINE_BIND_POINT_COMPUTE, k->pl, 0, 1, &ds, 0, 0);
}

void mda_vkc_dispatch(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_kernel_t *k, mda_vkc_buf_t **bufs, uint32_t gx, uint32_t gy, uint32_t gz, void *push) {
    VkDescriptorSetAllocateInfo dsa = {
        .sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_SET_ALLOCATE_INFO,
        .descriptorPool = c->rec_pool,
        .descriptorSetCount = 1,
        .pSetLayouts = &k->dsl,
    };
    VkDescriptorSet ds = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    MDA_VKC_CK(c, vkAllocateDescriptorSets(c->dev, &dsa, &ds));
    if (c->fail == false) {
        mda_vkc_bind_set(c, k, bufs, ds);
    }
    if (c->fail == false && k->push_sz != 0) {
        vkCmdPushConstants(c->rec, k->pl, VK_SHADER_STAGE_COMPUTE_BIT, 0, k->push_sz, push);
    }
    if (c->fail == false) {
        vkCmdDispatch(c->rec, gx, gy, gz);
    }
}

void mda_vkc_finish(mda_vkc_t *c, bool oneshot, const char *name) {
    if (oneshot) {
        mda_vkc_flush(c);
    } else if (c->fail == false && c->nobar == false) {
        mda_vkc_barrier(c->rec);
        mda_vkc_prof_mark(c, name);
    }
}

void mda_vkc_run(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_kernel_t *k, mda_vkc_buf_t **bufs, uint32_t gx, uint32_t gy, uint32_t gz, void *push) {
    bool oneshot = c->recording == false;
    if (c->fail == false && (gx > c->max_groups[0] || gy > c->max_groups[1] || gz > c->max_groups[2])) {
        fprintf(stderr, "vkc: dispatch %ux%ux%u exceeds the device limit\n", gx, gy, gz);
        c->fail = true;
    }
    if (c->fail == false && oneshot) {
        mda_vkc_begin(c);
    }
    if (c->fail == false) {
        mda_vkc_dispatch(c, k, bufs, gx, gy, gz, push);
        mda_vkc_finish(c, oneshot, k->name);
    }
}

void mda_vkc_copy(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_buf_t *dst, mda_vkc_buf_t *src, VkDeviceSize sz) {
    bool oneshot = c->recording == false;
    VkBufferCopy r = {
        .srcOffset = src->off,
        .dstOffset = dst->off,
        .size = sz,
    };
    if (c->fail == false && oneshot) {
        mda_vkc_begin(c);
    }
    if (c->fail == false) {
        vkCmdCopyBuffer(c->rec, src->buf, dst->buf, 1, &r);
        mda_vkc_finish(c, oneshot, "copy");
    }
}

void mda_vkc_fill(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_buf_t *b, uint32_t word) {
    bool oneshot = c->recording == false;
    if (c->fail == false && oneshot) {
        mda_vkc_begin(c);
    }
    if (c->fail == false) {
        vkCmdFillBuffer(c->rec, b->buf, b->off, b->sz, word);
        mda_vkc_finish(c, oneshot, "fill");
    }
}

void mda_vkc_set_nobar(mda_vkc_t *c, bool on) {
    c->nobar = on;
}

/// file: vkc.h
#ifndef MDA_VKC_H
#define MDA_VKC_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <vulkan/vulkan.h>

#define MDA_VKC_CK(c, x) mda_vkc_check((c), (x), __FILE__, __LINE__)
#define MDA_VKC_MAX_BINDINGS 24
#define MDA_VKC_MAX_LABELS 64
#define MDA_VKC_MAX_QUERIES 16384
#define MDA_VKC_NO_FAMILY 0xffffffffu

typedef struct mda_vkc_t mda_vkc_t;
typedef struct mda_vkc_buf_t mda_vkc_buf_t;
typedef struct mda_vkc_kernel_t mda_vkc_kernel_t;
typedef struct mda_vkc_cmd_t mda_vkc_cmd_t;
typedef struct mda_vkc_spv_t mda_vkc_spv_t;

struct mda_vkc_t {
    VkInstance inst;
    VkPhysicalDevice pd;
    VkDevice dev;
    VkQueue q;
    VkCommandPool cpool;
    VkDescriptorPool dpool;
    VkCommandBuffer rec;
    VkDescriptorPool rec_pool;
    VkQueryPool qpool;
    const char **qname;
    const char *prof_name[MDA_VKC_MAX_LABELS];
    double prof_ms[MDA_VKC_MAX_LABELS];
    uint32_t prof_count[MDA_VKC_MAX_LABELS];
    uint32_t max_groups[3];
    uint32_t qfi;
    uint32_t mem_dev;
    uint32_t mem_cached;
    uint32_t nq;
    uint32_t prof_n;
    float ts_period;
    bool recording;
    bool nobar;
    bool prof;
    bool prof_rec;
    bool fail;
};

struct mda_vkc_buf_t {
    VkBuffer buf;
    VkDeviceMemory mem;
    void *ptr;
    VkDeviceSize off;
    VkDeviceSize sz;
};

struct mda_vkc_kernel_t {
    VkPipeline pipe;
    VkPipelineLayout pl;
    VkDescriptorSetLayout dsl;
    const char *name;
    uint32_t nbuf;
    uint32_t push_sz;
};

struct mda_vkc_cmd_t {
    VkCommandBuffer cb;
    VkDescriptorPool pool;
};

struct mda_vkc_spv_t {
    uint32_t *b;
    size_t len;
};

void mda_vkc_check(mda_vkc_t *c, VkResult r, const char *file, uint32_t line);
mda_vkc_t mda_vkc_init(void);
mda_vkc_buf_t mda_vkc_alloc_type(mda_vkc_t *c, VkDeviceSize sz, uint32_t type);
mda_vkc_buf_t mda_vkc_alloc(mda_vkc_t *c, VkDeviceSize sz);
mda_vkc_buf_t mda_vkc_alloc_cached(mda_vkc_t *c, VkDeviceSize sz);
void mda_vkc_free(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_buf_t *b);
mda_vkc_buf_t mda_vkc_view(mda_vkc_buf_t *b, VkDeviceSize off, VkDeviceSize sz);
mda_vkc_kernel_t mda_vkc_load(mda_vkc_t *c, const char *spv, uint32_t nbuf, uint32_t push_sz);
void mda_vkc_begin(mda_vkc_t *c);
void mda_vkc_prof_begin(mda_vkc_t *c);
void mda_vkc_prof_report(mda_vkc_t *c, uint32_t batches);
void mda_vkc_flush(mda_vkc_t *c);
mda_vkc_cmd_t mda_vkc_record(mda_vkc_t *c);
void mda_vkc_end(mda_vkc_t *c);
void mda_vkc_submit(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_cmd_t *cmd);
void mda_vkc_run(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_kernel_t *k, mda_vkc_buf_t **bufs, uint32_t gx, uint32_t gy, uint32_t gz, void *push);
void mda_vkc_copy(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_buf_t *dst, mda_vkc_buf_t *src, VkDeviceSize sz);
void mda_vkc_fill(mda_vkc_t *c, mda_vkc_buf_t *b, uint32_t word);
void mda_vkc_set_nobar(mda_vkc_t *c, bool on);

#endif

/// file: vocab.c
#include <math.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "vocab.h"
#include "hash.h"
#include "util.h"
#include "sort.h"

mda_vocab_text_t mda_vocab_none(void) {
    return (mda_vocab_text_t) {
        .bytes = NULL,
        .len = 0,
    };
}

size_t mda_vocab_pieces(size_t len, size_t n) {
    if (len > n) {
        return n / MDA_VOCAB_SAMPLE_PIECE;
    } else {
        return 1;
    }
}

size_t mda_vocab_piece(size_t len, size_t n) {
    if (len > n) {
        return MDA_VOCAB_SAMPLE_PIECE;
    } else {
        return len;
    }
}

mda_vocab_text_t mda_vocab_read_pieces(FILE *f, const char *path, size_t n) {
    fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END);
    size_t len = (size_t)ftell(f);
    size_t pieces = mda_vocab_pieces(len, n);
    size_t piece = mda_vocab_piece(len, n);
    size_t stride = len / pieces;
    uint8_t *b = malloc(pieces * piece);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < pieces; i += 1) {
        int32_t sought = fseek(f, (int64_t)(i * stride), SEEK_SET);
        size_t got = fread(b + i * piece, 1, piece, f);
        if (sought != 0 || got != piece) {
            fprintf(stderr, "%s: short read\n", path);
            fclose(f);
            free(b);
            return mda_vocab_none();
        }
    }
    fclose(f);
    fprintf(stderr, "sample: %zu bytes from %zu pieces of %s (%zu bytes)\n", pieces * piece, pieces, path, len);
    return (mda_vocab_text_t) {
        .bytes = b,
        .len = pieces * piece,
    };
}

mda_vocab_text_t mda_vocab_sample(const char *path, size_t n) {
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "rb");
    if (f == NULL) {
        perror(path);
        return mda_vocab_none();
    } else {
        return mda_vocab_read_pieces(f, path, n);
    }
}

uint32_t mda_vocab_byte_class(uint8_t b) {
    if (b == ' ' || b == '\n' || b == '\t' || b == '\r') {
        return 0;
    } else if (b >= '0' && b <= '9') {
        return 1;
    } else if (((b | 32) >= 'a' && (b | 32) <= 'z') || b >= 0x80) {
        return 2;
    } else {
        return 3;
    }
}

uint32_t mda_vocab_lead(const uint8_t *t, uint32_t max) {
    if (max > 1 && t[0] == ' ' && mda_vocab_byte_class(t[1]) != 0) {
        return 1;
    } else {
        return 0;
    }
}

uint32_t mda_vocab_shape_len(const uint8_t *t, uint32_t max) {
    uint32_t s = mda_vocab_lead(t, max);
    uint32_t c = mda_vocab_byte_class(t[s]);
    uint32_t l = s + 1;
    while (l < max && mda_vocab_byte_class(t[l]) == c) {
        l += 1;
    }
    return l;
}

mda_vocab_map_t mda_vocab_map_make(uint32_t cap) {
    return (mda_vocab_map_t) {
        .key = malloc((size_t)cap * sizeof(mda_vocab_key_t)),
        .val = malloc((size_t)cap * sizeof(uint32_t)),
        .len = calloc(cap, 1),
        .cap = cap,
        .used = 0,
    };
}

void mda_vocab_map_free(mda_vocab_map_t *t) {
    free(t->key);
    free(t->val);
    free(t->len);
}

mda_vocab_key_t mda_vocab_key(const char *s, uint32_t len) {
    mda_vocab_key_t k = {
        .lo = 0,
        .hi = 0,
    };
    memcpy(&k, s, len);
    return k;
}

uint32_t mda_vocab_map_slot0(const mda_vocab_map_t *t, mda_vocab_key_t k, uint32_t len) {
    uint64_t x = mda_hash_mix(k.lo + (uint64_t)len * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ull);
    return (uint32_t)mda_hash_mix(x ^ k.hi) & (t->cap - 1);
}

uint32_t mda_vocab_map_find(const mda_vocab_map_t *t, mda_vocab_key_t k, uint32_t len) {
    uint32_t slot = mda_vocab_map_slot0(t, k, len);
    while (t->len[slot] != 0 && (t->len[slot] != len || t->key[slot].lo != k.lo || t->key[slot].hi != k.hi)) {
        slot = (slot + 1) & (t->cap - 1);
    }
    return slot;
}

void mda_vocab_map_grow(mda_vocab_map_t *t) {
    mda_vocab_map_t g = mda_vocab_map_make(t->cap * 2);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < t->cap; i += 1) {
        if (t->len[i] != 0) {
            uint32_t slot = mda_vocab_map_find(&g, t->key[i], t->len[i]);
            g.key[slot] = t->key[i];
            g.val[slot] = t->val[i];
            g.len[slot] = t->len[i];
        }
    }
    g.used = t->used;
    mda_vocab_map_free(t);
    *t = g;
}

uint32_t *mda_vocab_map_put(mda_vocab_map_t *t, const char *s, uint32_t len) {
    if ((t->used + 1) * 2 > t->cap) {
        mda_vocab_map_grow(t);
    }
    mda_vocab_key_t k = mda_vocab_key(s, len);
    uint32_t slot = mda_vocab_map_find(t, k, len);
    if (t->len[slot] == 0) {
        t->key[slot] = k;
        t->val[slot] = 0;
        t->len[slot] = (uint8_t)len;
        t->used += 1;
    }
    return &t->val[slot];
}

int32_t mda_vocab_map_get(const mda_vocab_map_t *t, const char *s, uint32_t len) {
    uint32_t slot = mda_vocab_map_find(t, mda_vocab_key(s, len), len);
    if (t->len[slot] == 0) {
        return -1;
    } else {
        return (int32_t)t->val[slot];
    }
}

mda_vocab_map_t mda_vocab_count(const mda_vocab_text_t *t) {
    mda_vocab_map_t h = mda_vocab_map_make(1u << 22);
    fprintf(stderr, "seeding: counting substrings (len 2..%u) over %zu bytes...\n", MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN, t->len);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < t->len; i += 1) {
        uint32_t top = mda_vocab_shape_len(t->bytes + i, (uint32_t)mda_util_min_size(t->len - i, MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN));
        for (uint32_t l = 2; l <= top; l += 1) {
            uint32_t *count = mda_vocab_map_put(&h, (const char *)t->bytes + i, l);
            *count += 1;
        }
    }
    return h;
}

bool mda_vocab_seen_twice(const mda_vocab_map_t *h, uint32_t slot) {
    return h->len[slot] != 0 && h->val[slot] >= 2;
}

uint32_t mda_vocab_pool_size(const mda_vocab_map_t *h) {
    uint32_t n = 0;
    for (uint32_t slot = 0; slot < h->cap; slot += 1) {
        if (mda_vocab_seen_twice(h, slot)) {
            n += 1;
        }
    }
    return n;
}

mda_sort_item_t *mda_vocab_pool(const mda_vocab_map_t *h, uint32_t n) {
    mda_sort_item_t *pool = malloc((size_t)n * sizeof(mda_sort_item_t));
    uint32_t k = 0;
    for (uint32_t slot = 0; slot < h->cap; slot += 1) {
        if (mda_vocab_seen_twice(h, slot)) {
            pool[k] = (mda_sort_item_t) {
                .key = -(double)h->val[slot] * h->len[slot],
                .idx = slot,
            };
            k += 1;
        }
    }
    mda_sort_items(pool, n);
    return pool;
}

mda_vocab_cands_t mda_vocab_seed(const mda_vocab_text_t *t) {
    mda_vocab_map_t h = mda_vocab_count(t);
    uint32_t pool_n = mda_vocab_pool_size(&h);
    fprintf(stderr, "seeding: %u distinct substrings, %u seen twice or more, keeping top %u + 256 bytes\n", h.used, pool_n, MDA_VOCAB_SEED_KEEP);
    mda_sort_item_t *pool = mda_vocab_pool(&h, pool_n);
    uint32_t keep = mda_util_min_u32(pool_n, MDA_VOCAB_SEED_KEEP);
    mda_vocab_cand_t *v = malloc((MDA_VOCAB_SEED_KEEP + 256) * sizeof(mda_vocab_cand_t));
    for (uint32_t b = 0; b < 256; b += 1) {
        v[b] = (mda_vocab_cand_t) {
            .logp = 0.0,
            .expected = 1.0,
            .score = 0.0,
            .len = 1,
        };
        v[b].bytes[0] = (char)b;
    }
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < keep; i += 1) {
        uint32_t slot = pool[i].idx;
        v[256 + i] = (mda_vocab_cand_t) {
            .logp = 0.0,
            .expected = h.val[slot],
            .score = -pool[i].key,
            .len = h.len[slot],
        };
        memcpy(v[256 + i].bytes, &h.key[slot], h.len[slot]);
    }
    free(pool);
    mda_vocab_map_free(&h);
    uint32_t n = 256 + keep;
    double total = 0.0;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; i += 1) {
        total += v[i].expected;
    }
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; i += 1) {
        v[i].logp = log(v[i].expected / total);
    }
    return (mda_vocab_cands_t) {
        .v = v,
        .n = n,
    };
}

mda_vocab_map_t mda_vocab_lookup(const mda_vocab_cands_t *c) {
    mda_vocab_map_t lk = mda_vocab_map_make(1u << 16);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < c->n; i += 1) {
        *mda_vocab_map_put(&lk, c->v[i].bytes, c->v[i].len) = i;
    }
    return lk;
}

bool mda_vocab_reached(double x) {
    return x > MDA_VOCAB_NEG_INF + 1.0;
}

double mda_vocab_logaddexp(double a, double b) {
    if (mda_vocab_reached(a)) {
        double hi = fmax(a, b);
        return hi + log(exp(a - hi) + exp(b - hi));
    } else {
        return b;
    }
}

int32_t mda_vocab_chunk_id(const mda_vocab_map_t *lk, const uint8_t *text, uint32_t top, uint32_t l) {
    if (l <= top) {
        return mda_vocab_map_get(lk, (const char *)text, l);
    } else {
        return -1;
    }
}

int32_t *mda_vocab_chunk_ids(const mda_vocab_map_t *lk, const uint8_t *text, uint32_t len) {
    int32_t *ids = malloc((size_t)len * MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN * sizeof(int32_t));
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len; i += 1) {
        uint32_t top = mda_vocab_shape_len(text + i, mda_util_min_u32(len - i, MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN));
        for (uint32_t l = 1; l <= MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN; l += 1) {
            ids[i * MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN + l - 1] = mda_vocab_chunk_id(lk, text + i, top, l);
        }
    }
    return ids;
}

double *mda_vocab_forward(const mda_vocab_cands_t *c, const int32_t *ids, uint32_t len) {
    double *alpha = malloc((len + 1) * sizeof(double));
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i <= len; i += 1) {
        alpha[i] = MDA_VOCAB_NEG_INF;
    }
    alpha[0] = 0.0;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len; i += 1) {
        for (uint32_t l = 1; l <= MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN && mda_vocab_reached(alpha[i]); l += 1) {
            int32_t id = ids[i * MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN + l - 1];
            if (id >= 0) {
                alpha[i + l] = mda_vocab_logaddexp(alpha[i + l], alpha[i] + c->v[id].logp);
            }
        }
    }
    return alpha;
}

double *mda_vocab_backward(const mda_vocab_cands_t *c, const int32_t *ids, uint32_t len) {
    double *beta = malloc((len + 1) * sizeof(double));
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i <= len; i += 1) {
        beta[i] = MDA_VOCAB_NEG_INF;
    }
    beta[len] = 0.0;
    for (uint32_t ii = len; ii > 0; ii -= 1) {
        uint32_t i = ii - 1;
        for (uint32_t l = 1; l <= MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN; l += 1) {
            int32_t id = ids[i * MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN + l - 1];
            if (id >= 0 && mda_vocab_reached(beta[i + l])) {
                beta[i] = mda_vocab_logaddexp(beta[i], c->v[id].logp + beta[i + l]);
            }
        }
    }
    return beta;
}

void mda_vocab_posterior(mda_vocab_cands_t *c, const int32_t *ids, const double *alpha, const double *beta, uint32_t len) {
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len; i += 1) {
        for (uint32_t l = 1; l <= MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN && mda_vocab_reached(alpha[i]); l += 1) {
            int32_t id = ids[i * MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN + l - 1];
            if (id >= 0) {
                c->v[id].expected += exp(alpha[i] + c->v[id].logp + beta[i + l] - alpha[len]);
            }
        }
    }
}

double mda_vocab_em_chunk(mda_vocab_cands_t *c, const mda_vocab_map_t *lk, const uint8_t *text, uint32_t len) {
    int32_t *ids = mda_vocab_chunk_ids(lk, text, len);
    double *alpha = mda_vocab_forward(c, ids, len);
    double total = alpha[len];
    if (mda_vocab_reached(total)) {
        double *beta = mda_vocab_backward(c, ids, len);
        mda_vocab_posterior(c, ids, alpha, beta, len);
        free(beta);
    }
    free(ids);
    free(alpha);
    if (mda_vocab_reached(total)) {
        return total;
    } else {
        return 0.0;
    }
}

void mda_vocab_em_round(mda_vocab_cands_t *c, const mda_vocab_text_t *t, const mda_vocab_map_t *lk) {
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < c->n; i += 1) {
        c->v[i].expected = 0.0;
    }
    double ll = 0.0;
    for (size_t done = 0; done < t->len; done += MDA_VOCAB_EM_CHUNK) {
        ll += mda_vocab_em_chunk(c, lk, t->bytes + done, (uint32_t)mda_util_min_size(t->len - done, MDA_VOCAB_EM_CHUNK));
    }
    double total = 0.0;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < c->n; i += 1) {
        total += c->v[i].expected;
    }
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < c->n; i += 1) {
        c->v[i].logp = log(fmax(c->v[i].expected, 1e-9) / total);
    }
    fprintf(stderr, "EM round: %.4f bits/byte, %.3f bytes/token, vocab %u\n", -ll / t->len / log(2.0), t->len / total, c->n);
}

void mda_vocab_em(mda_vocab_cands_t *c, const mda_vocab_text_t *t) {
    mda_vocab_map_t lk = mda_vocab_lookup(c);
    for (uint32_t it = 0; it < MDA_VOCAB_EM_ITERS; it += 1) {
        mda_vocab_em_round(c, t, &lk);
    }
    mda_vocab_map_free(&lk);
}

double mda_vocab_split_logp(const mda_vocab_cands_t *c, const mda_vocab_map_t *lk, const mda_vocab_cand_t *cand) {
    double best[MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN + 1];
    best[0] = 0.0;
    for (uint32_t p = 1; p <= cand->len; p += 1) {
        best[p] = MDA_VOCAB_NEG_INF;
    }
    for (uint32_t s = 0; s < cand->len; s += 1) {
        for (uint32_t l = 1; s + l <= cand->len; l += 1) {
            int32_t id = mda_vocab_map_get(lk, cand->bytes + s, l);
            if (id >= 0 && (s != 0 || l != cand->len)) {
                best[s + l] = fmax(best[s + l], best[s] + c->v[id].logp);
            }
        }
    }
    return best[cand->len];
}

void mda_vocab_prune_scores(mda_vocab_cands_t *c, const mda_vocab_map_t *lk) {
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < c->n; i += 1) {
        mda_vocab_cand_t *cand = &c->v[i];
        if (cand->len > 1) {
            cand->score = cand->expected * (cand->logp - mda_vocab_split_logp(c, lk, cand));
        }
    }
}

double mda_vocab_prune_key(const mda_vocab_cand_t *cand) {
    if (cand->len == 1) {
        return INFINITY;
    } else {
        return cand->score;
    }
}

void mda_vocab_prune(mda_vocab_cands_t *c, uint32_t target) {
    mda_vocab_map_t lk = mda_vocab_lookup(c);
    mda_vocab_prune_scores(c, &lk);
    mda_vocab_map_free(&lk);
    mda_sort_item_t *order = malloc(c->n * sizeof(mda_sort_item_t));
    uint32_t multibyte = 0;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < c->n; i += 1) {
        order[i] = (mda_sort_item_t) {
            .key = mda_vocab_prune_key(&c->v[i]),
            .idx = i,
        };
        if (c->v[i].len > 1) {
            multibyte += 1;
        }
    }
    mda_sort_items(order, c->n);
    uint32_t keep = mda_util_min_u32(mda_util_max_u32((uint32_t)((double)c->n * MDA_VOCAB_PRUNE_KEEP), target), c->n);
    uint32_t drop = mda_util_min_u32(c->n - keep, multibyte);
    mda_vocab_cand_t *kept = malloc((c->n - drop) * sizeof(mda_vocab_cand_t));
    for (uint32_t r = drop; r < c->n; r += 1) {
        kept[r - drop] = c->v[order[r].idx];
    }
    free(order);
    free(c->v);
    c->v = kept;
    c->n -= drop;
}

bool mda_vocab_write(const char *path, const mda_vocab_cands_t *c) {
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "w");
    if (f == NULL) {
        perror(path);
        return false;
    } else {
        fprintf(f, "%u\n", c->n);
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < c->n; i += 1) {
            mda_tokenizer_write_entry(f, (float)c->v[i].logp, c->v[i].bytes, c->v[i].len);
        }
        int32_t closed = fclose(f);
        if (closed != 0) {
            perror(path);
        } else {
            fprintf(stderr, "wrote %u tokens to %s\n", c->n, path);
        }
        return closed == 0;
    }
}

/// file: vocab.h
#ifndef MDA_VOCAB_H
#define MDA_VOCAB_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>

#include "tokenizer.h"

#define MDA_VOCAB_EM_CHUNK 4096
#define MDA_VOCAB_EM_ITERS 3
#define MDA_VOCAB_EM_SAMPLE_BYTES (16 * 1024 * 1024)
#define MDA_VOCAB_NEG_INF (-1e30)
#define MDA_VOCAB_PRUNE_KEEP 0.75
#define MDA_VOCAB_SAMPLE_PIECE (1024 * 1024)
#define MDA_VOCAB_SEED_KEEP 200000
#define MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN 16
#define MDA_VOCAB_SEED_SAMPLE_BYTES (64 * 1024 * 1024)
#define MDA_VOCAB_TARGET 16000

_Static_assert(MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN <= 16 && MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN <= MDA_TOKENIZER_MAX_LEN, "keys must fit 16 bytes and the runtime");

typedef struct mda_vocab_text_t mda_vocab_text_t;
typedef struct mda_vocab_cand_t mda_vocab_cand_t;
typedef struct mda_vocab_cands_t mda_vocab_cands_t;
typedef struct mda_vocab_key_t mda_vocab_key_t;
typedef struct mda_vocab_map_t mda_vocab_map_t;

struct mda_vocab_text_t {
    uint8_t *bytes;
    size_t len;
};

struct mda_vocab_cand_t {
    double logp;
    double expected;
    double score;
    char bytes[MDA_VOCAB_SEED_MAX_LEN];
    uint32_t len;
};

struct mda_vocab_cands_t {
    mda_vocab_cand_t *v;
    uint32_t n;
};

struct mda_vocab_key_t {
    uint64_t lo;
    uint64_t hi;
};

struct mda_vocab_map_t {
    mda_vocab_key_t *key;
    uint32_t *val;
    uint8_t *len;
    uint32_t cap;
    uint32_t used;
};

mda_vocab_text_t mda_vocab_sample(const char *path, size_t n);
mda_vocab_cands_t mda_vocab_seed(const mda_vocab_text_t *t);
void mda_vocab_em(mda_vocab_cands_t *c, const mda_vocab_text_t *t);
void mda_vocab_prune(mda_vocab_cands_t *c, uint32_t target);
bool mda_vocab_write(const char *path, const mda_vocab_cands_t *c);

#endif
